TL;DR: 本文提出不完备多模态情感分析中的新问题——未见模态组合（IMSAUMC），并设计了对比混合提示学习模型（CMPL），通过标签引导的对比特征学习、软路由模态组合提示及三种提示对比学习策略，在三个基准数据集上超越现有方法5%以上的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不完备多模态情感分析方法通常假设数据随机缺失或针对特定缺失模式设计，忽略了训练与测试阶段模态组合不一致的问题。真实场景中测试阶段常遇到训练阶段未出现的模态组合，导致泛化能力不足和性能不稳定。

Method: 提出CMPL模型，包含三个核心组件：(1) 标签引导的对比特征学习机制，学习鲁棒且具有判别性的跨模态表示；(2) 带软路由的模态组合提示设计，促进不同模态组合的学习；(3) 三种提示对比学习策略，有效学习未见模态组合对应的提示，增强模型在多样化测试场景中的泛化能力。

Result: 在三个广泛使用的数据集上进行的大量实验表明，CMPL相比最先进的方法在准确率上提升了超过5%。

Conclusion: CMPL有效解决了不完备多模态情感分析中未见模态组合的泛化难题，通过对比学习与提示学习的结合显著提升了模型在未见模态组合场景下的鲁棒性和性能。

Abstract: Incomplete multimodal sentiment analysis has garnered significant attention in recent years. Existing approaches typically assume that data is missing at random or are designed specifically for certain missing patterns, ignoring the modality combination inconsistency between training and testing phases. However, in real-world scenarios, the testing phase often encounters modal combinations that were not present during the training phase, which leads to insufficient generalization capabilities and unstable performance. In this paper, we introduce the problem of Incomplete Multimodal Sentiment Analysis with Unseen Modality Combinations (IMSAUMC), aiming to enhance model generalization for unseen modality combinations. To address this challenge, we propose the model named $\textbf{C}$ontrastive $\textbf{M}$ixed $\textbf{P}$rompt $\textbf{L}$earning ($\textsf{CMPL}$) for IMSAUMC. It introduces a label-guided contrastive feature learning mechanism to learn robust and discriminative cross-modal representations. Additionally, we design modality-combination prompts with a soft router to facilitate better learning of various modality combinations. Furthermore, we introduce three prompt contrastive learning strategies, which enable effective learning of prompts corresponding to unseen modality combinations, thereby significantly strengthening the model's generalization capabilities in diverse testing scenarios. Extensive experiments on three widely used datasets demonstrate that $\textsf{CMPL}$ achieves more than a 5% improvement in accuracy compared to state-of-the-art approaches.

</details>


### [34] [A three-dimensional typology of agency for advanced AI systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20041)
*Willem Fourie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文基于哲学、伦理学、法律理论和社会学中已有的理论立场，为前沿AI系统开发了一个由三个维度（道德/法律性质、个体/集体模式、人类/非人类所在位置）组成的能动性类型学，组合产生八种可能的能动性实例，并将其分类为常规、有争议或存在争议。该类型学将法律能动性与道德能动性分离，为考虑个体、法律、非人类能动性创造了概念空间，而无需预设先进AI系统是道德主体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要将AI能动性视为规范性概念，或聚焦于特别具有能动性的AI系统。虽然近期工作也关注能动性系统的不同画像，但缺乏一个能够解决先进AI系统所体现的能动性类型问题的框架，尤其是考虑非道德形式的能动性时更是如此。

Method: 基于哲学、伦理学、法律理论和社会学中已确立的理论立场，构建了一个包含三个维度的前沿AI系统能动性类型学：能动性的性质（道德或法律）、能动性的模式（个体或集体）和能动性的所在位置（人类或非人类）。将三个维度组合产生八种可能的能动性实例，并进一步将其分类为常规、有争议或存在争议三类。

Result: 该类型学成功将法律能动性与道德能动性区分开来，从而为考虑个体、法律、非人类的能动性创造了概念空间，而不必预先假定先进AI系统是道德主体。

Conclusion: 作者论证指出，在工具性目标追求使AI行为归因于特定人类行为者的过程复杂化的情况下，法律能动性与道德能动性的区分正变得越来越重要。

Abstract: Research on the agency of advanced artificial intelligence (AI) systems focuses on agency as a normative concept and on the agency of particularly agentic AI systems. While recent work also focuses on the different profiles of agentic systems, no framework exists to address the question of the type of agency instantiated by advanced AI systems, particularly when considering non-moral forms of agency. Based on established theoretical positions in philosophy, ethics, legal theory and sociology, we develop a typology of agency for frontier AI systems consisting of three dimensions: the nature of agency (moral or legal), its mode (individual or collective) and its locus (human or non-human). Combining these dimensions produces eight possible instantiations of agency, which we classify as conventional, contested or controversial. The typology separates legal from moral agency and thereby creates conceptual space for considering individual, legal, non-human agency without presupposing that advanced AI systems are moral agents. We argue that this distinction is increasingly relevant where instrumental goal pursuit complicates the attribution of AI actions to particular human actors.

</details>


### [35] [On the Applicability of Safety Nets: A Safety-By-Design Solution for Certifying Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20053)
*Johann Maximilian Christensen,Thomas Stefani,Elena Hoemann,Frank Köster,Sven Hallerbach*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次系统分析了Safety Nets中神经网络与查找表大小之间的权衡关系，通过对比不同架构的神经网络，确定3-5个隐藏层、每层50-100个节点并结合one-hot编码的设计参数为最优配置。该配置下神经网络可准确表示至少97%的数据，紧凑查找表处理剩余误差，系统大小缩减近三个数量级，满足当前航电硬件内存预算，并保证整个离散化输入空间100%正确输出，符合EASA的Safety-by-Design认证要求。研究还提供了首个开源的HCAS和VCAS Safety Nets实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在航空等安全关键系统中集成AI面临重大认证和部署挑战。EASA要求采用Safety-by-Design方法，可通过结合神经网络压缩与查找表的Safety Nets实现。然而此前缺乏对Safety Nets性能特征和系统设计权衡的综合研究，亟需系统性分析以确定最优设计参数。

Method: 通过系统比较具有不同架构的神经网络，分析神经网络与查找表大小之间的权衡关系，寻找在满足认证合规性前提下最小化整体存储和内存需求的最优设计参数。评估了不同隐藏层数量、每层节点数以及编码方式（如one-hot编码）对系统性能的影响。

Result: 实验结果表明，3至5个隐藏层、每层50至100个节点并采用one-hot编码的架构达到最佳平衡，神经网络可准确表示至少97%的数据，紧凑查找表处理其余误差。Safety Nets将系统大小缩减近三个数量级，适配当前航电硬件内存预算，同时在离散化输入空间上保证100%正确输出。

Conclusion: 本研究为Safety Nets提供了首个全面的性能特征分析，并发布首个开源实现（HCAS和VCAS），结果可复现。该工作证明Safety Nets是安全关键应用中可行的Safety-by-Design解决方案，为航空领域可认证AI系统开辟了实用路径。

Abstract: The integration of Artificial Intelligence (AI) in safety-critical aviation systems presents significant challenges for certification and deployment. Aviation, often regarded as the safest form of transportation, relies on numerous safety-critical systems. For future safety-critical AI-based systems, EASA requires a Safety-by-Design approach, which can be achieved by using Safety Nets that combine neural network compression with lookup tables to ensure 100 % correct runtime behavior across the discretized operational design domain. Although Safety Nets have been studied, no comprehensive study of their performance characteristics and system design trade-offs has been conducted. This work presents the first systematic analysis of the trade-off between neural network and lookup table size in Safety Nets. By systematically comparing neural networks with diverse architectures, this study identifies optimal design parameters that minimize overall storage and memory requirements while maintaining certification compliance. Results demonstrate that architectures with 3 to 5 hidden layers, each with approximately 50 to 100 nodes, combined with one-hot encoding, achieve the best balance. In these configurations, neural networks accurately represent at least 97 % of the data, while compact lookup tables handle the remaining errors. The resulting Safety Nets reduce the system size by almost three orders of magnitude, fitting within the memory budget of current avionics hardware while guaranteeing 100 % correct outputs across the entire discretized input space, as required by EASA guidelines. This work provides the first-ever open-source implementation of Safety Nets for HCAS and VCAS with replicable results, demonstrating a practical pathway toward certifiable AI-based systems in aviation and establishing Safety Nets as a viable Safety-by-Design solution for safety-critical applications.

</details>


### [36] [What You Can't See Is What You Learn: Restricted Evidence Visibility Favors Compositional Generalization in Shared-Genome Language-Model Societies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20054)
*Narcis Marincat*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在多模块系统中，将输入可见性限制（restricted visibility）到特定模块，相比全局可见（global visibility）配置，能显著提高梯度训练发现可泛化组合中继的概率。在冻结预训练语言模型加低秩适配器的四细胞社会中，受限组在10对匹配实验中有9对以至少20个点的优势超越全局组，且其通信载体为可复用、值索引的接口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模块系统中每个模块都暴露于全部输入，作者想验证限制证据可见性是否会改变梯度训练发现的不同解决方案，以探索模块化系统在功能组合任务上更好的学习机制。

Method: 构建四细胞社会：共享一个冻结预训练语言模型和一个低秩适配器，仅通过两个模型宽度的连续向量在固定中继中通信。在密封（预先不可见）的自然语言函数组合任务上，训练十对匹配的受限/全局可见系统对，二者共享初始化字节、训练顺序、令牌布局、参数与计算，仅注意力掩码不同。还进行了消融（切断通信）、函数移植、破坏性干预和反事实数据包实验。

Result: 受限社会在9/10对中以至少20点优势优于全局可见孪生体，深度2和深度3的中位配对优势分别为0.7648和0.6050。切断通信使所有受限社会降至随机水平；深度3优势在组合函数从未出现在训练中的程序上仍保持0.558。同值数据包移植在6个审计的受限社会中保持行为0.94-1.00；破坏性干预导致性能崩溃；反事实数据包将输出引向数学预测答案。但唯一性能优异的全局模型的数据包在不同环节间不可互换。受限臂中位深度3准确率为0.6988，低于0.70门槛，完整预注册测试失败。

Conclusion: 受限可见性并非组合能力的必要条件，但在此实验方案下它大幅提高了泛化中继的出现概率，并催生可重用、值索引的接口。预注册测试正式未通过（中位准确率0.6988低于0.70下限），且早先的资格队列中所有十个模型均未通过普通语言保留测试，系统只能用于明确任务门控的场景。

Abstract: Multi-module systems often expose every module to the full input. We test whether restricting evidence visibility changes which solutions gradient-based training discovers. Four-cell societies share one frozen pretrained language model and one low-rank adapter, communicating only through two model-width continuous vectors in a fixed relay. On a prospectively sealed natural-language function-composition task, we train ten matched restricted/global pairs sharing initialization bytes, training order, token layout, parameters, and computation; only the attention mask differs. Restricted societies outperform their globally visible twins by at least 20 points at both depths in 9 of 10 pairs, with median paired advantages of 0.7648 and 0.6050. Cutting communication reduces every restricted society to chance, and the depth-three advantage remains 0.558 on programs whose composite function never appeared in training. Across six audited restricted societies, same-value packet transplants preserve behavior at 0.94-1.00 across all tested interfaces; destructive interventions collapse performance; and counterfactual packets redirect outputs toward the mathematically predicted answer. The sole high-performing global model also requires communication, but its same-value packets are not interchangeable across episodes. Restricted visibility is thus not necessary for composition; under this protocol it substantially increases the probability of a generalizing relay and favors a reusable, value-indexed interface. The complete preregistered battery nevertheless formally fails because restricted-arm median depth-three accuracy is 0.6988, below the 0.70 floor. An earlier qualification cohort likewise yielded 0/10 complete passes: one model met every task-performance gate, but all ten failed ordinary-language preservation, confining the system to explicitly task-gated use.

</details>

*Siyuan Ma,Boshi Zhang,Yutian Zhang,Qinglian Wu,Jiaqi Zhai,Dong Wei,Qiaojun Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动操作机器人需要预测运动与手臂动作如何联合改变未来观测和控制，但现有世界动作模型主要针对固定基座平台，未显式区分相机自运动与底盘、手臂动作对观测的影响，制约了移动平台上的视觉预测与全身控制效果。

Result: 在固定回放协议下，DECOWAM相比FastWAM同时提升了未来视频和动作预测，动作MSE降低21.7%，仅用25.95M可训练适配参数。每个方法进行79次闭环试验，DECOWAM在全身协调性和底盘位移鲁棒性上达到最高，任务完成率与最强基线相当。

Conclusion: 结果表明，具身感知的因式分解能够以参数高效的方式支持移动视角下的联合视觉预测与全身控制。

Abstract: Mobile manipulation requires a robot to predict how locomotion and arm motion jointly alter future observations and control. Existing world-action models, developed largely for fixed-base platforms, do not explicitly distinguish camera ego-motion from base and arm actions. Here we introduce DECOWAM, a whole-body world-action model that separates these factors through dedicated conditional interfaces. DECOWAM freezes an adapted FastWAM backbone and trains residual adapters, an action-equivalent future bottleneck distilled from privileged observations, adversarially separated base and arm latents, and base-velocity conditioning for video prediction. We further introduce ARMDOG, a real-robot dataset that synchronizes video, whole-body state and action, and language. On a fixed replay protocol, DECOWAM improved both future-video and action prediction over FastWAM, reducing action MSE by 21.7% with 25.95M trainable adaptation parameters. Across 79 closed-loop trials per method, it achieved the highest observed whole-body coordination and base-displacement robustness among the compared systems, while task completion remained comparable to the strongest baseline. These results show that embodiment-aware factorization can support parameter-efficient joint visual prediction and whole-body control under moving viewpoints.

</details>


### [38] [DARS: Dual-Level Credit Assignment RL with Structured Reasoning for Instruction-Based Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20161)
*Haoxiang Cao,Jiajiong Cao,Xuanpu Zhang,Changqian Yu,Chaoqun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DARS是一个用于指令式图像编辑的强化学习框架，针对planner-renderer两阶段流水线提出了双层级信用分配方法：在模块间通过多计划多渲染rollout估计奖励变异性实现软模块路由和自适应课程学习；在planner内部通过四字段结构化推理输出实现前缀门控奖励和token级优势重加权，将结果级反馈转化为局部监督。在五个基准上超越同配置的Joint RL基线，在推理密集型编辑上提升最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有指令式图像编辑系统采用planner-renderer流水线（VLM生成编辑计划，扩散模型执行计划）。仅用最终图像奖励训练这类系统效率低下，因为差的编辑结果无法揭示优化应更多归因于planner还是renderer，且即使确定是planner的问题，在自由形式的推理轨迹中也难以定位具体错误位置。亟需一种能够在两个模块间以及planner内部进行细粒度信用分配的方法。

Method: 提出DARS框架，包含两个层面的信用分配：(1) 跨模块层面：通过多计划多渲染rollout估计计划间与计划内奖励变异性，用于软模块路由（soft module routing）以决定优化重心；同时用rollout平均奖励估计难度，驱动自适应课程学习。(2) planner内部：设计四字段结构化推理输出，实现前缀门控奖励（prefix-gated reward）和token级优势重加权，将离散的结果级反馈转化为连续、可定位的局部监督信号。

Result: 在五个基准数据集上的实验表明，DARS优于使用相同骨干网络、数据、奖励模型和rollout预算的Joint RL基线，并且在推理密集型编辑任务上取得了最大的性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过双层级信用分配（模块间路由+模块内token级信用定位），可以有效解决两阶段编辑系统中奖励信用归属模糊的问题，显著提升训练效率和最终性能，尤其在需要复杂推理的编辑场景中优势明显。

Abstract: Instruction-based image editing uses a planner-renderer pipeline: a vision-language model (VLM) first converts the instruction into an edit plan, and a diffusion model then executes that plan. Training such systems with only final-image rewards is inefficient because a poor edit does not reveal whether additional optimization should place more emphasis on the planner or the renderer, and even planner-dominant cases remain difficult to localize within a free-form reasoning trace. We present DARS, a reinforcement learning framework for dual-level credit assignment in this two-stage setting. Across modules, multi-plan multi-render rollouts estimate between-plan and within-plan reward variability for soft module routing, while rollout mean rewards provide hardness estimates for an adaptive curriculum. Within the planner, a four-field structured reasoning output enables a prefix-gated reward and token-level advantage reweighting, turning outcome-level feedback into localized supervision. Experiments on five benchmarks show that DARS outperforms a Joint~RL baseline with the same backbone, data, reward model, and rollout budget, with the largest gains on reasoning-intensive edits.

</details>


### [39] [The Third Restructuring of Software Form: From the Three-Tier Architecture to Storage, Models, and Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20201)
*Wei Lin,Tao Zhou,Zhaofei Xie,Changgui Hong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出软件形态正在经历第三次范式转移——"软件3.0"，即由上下文和推理而非指令或数据驱动行为；并论证其终极形态收敛为三要素：广义数据库（所有持久状态与记忆的统一抽象）、大模型（执行推理与生成的智能核心）、以及智能体（连接前两者的执行循环）。在传统三层架构中，UI层将被模型按需生成界面所吸收，业务逻辑层按"可表达性×关键性"维度被重划分为模型推理与存储约束（残余确定性逻辑保留为工具），仅数据层升格为唯一持久基础设施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件形式经历了从指令驱动（软件1.0）到数据驱动（软件2.0/机器学习）的两次范式转移，本文提出第三次转移——由上下文和推理驱动的软件3.0——正在发生，并试图形式化这一收敛论点，明确其成立条件与失效边界。

Method: 本文形式化地提出了软件3.0的收敛论点，给出最小参考架构，并通过真实原型和在线模型的实证证据加以佐证，同时系统分析了确定性、成本、安全性和可验证性四个维度对该论点适用域的约束边界。

Result: 论文论证了该论点在"可表达、可验证、外部有状态、工具完备"的任务领域内成立，并指出它将重塑开发者角色、数据库产业与软件工程学科；同时明确了其不成立的边界条件。

Conclusion: 软件3.0代表软件形态的第三次范式转移，其收敛形态为"广义数据库+大模型+智能体"三要素架构，该论点在特定任务域内成立但对确定性、成本、安全性和可验证性敏感的领域仍存在边界限制。

Abstract: Software form has undergone two paradigm shifts since its inception: Software 1.0, in which instructions determine behavior, and Software 2.0, in which data determines behavior (machine learning). This paper argues that a third shift - Software 3.0, in which context and reasoning determine behavior - is now underway, and contends that its terminal form converges to three elements: a generalized database (the unified abstraction of all persistent state and memory), a large model (the intelligence core that performs reasoning and generation), and an agent (the execution loop connecting the first two). The core argument is as follows: in the traditional three-tier architecture, the user-interface layer will be absorbed by the model's ability to generate interfaces on demand, the business-logic layer will be re-partitioned along "expressibility x criticality" into model reasoning and storage constraints (with residual deterministic logic retained as tools), and only the data layer will be elevated into the sole persistent infrastructure. We formalize this convergence thesis, present a minimal reference architecture, report evidence from real prototypes and a live model, and systematically analyze both the conditions under which it holds and the boundaries where it fails - determinism, cost, security, and verifiability delimit the thesis's domain of applicability. We argue that the thesis holds in task domains that are expressible, verifiable, externally stateful, and tool-complete, and that it will reshape the roles of developers, the database industry, and the software-engineering discipline.

</details>


### [40] [MemTrapBench: Benchmarking Cognitive Traps in LLM Memory Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20202)
*Mengru Wang,Haozhe Luo,Zhenqian Xu,Zhixiang Cui,Haoming Xu,Qu Yang,Jizhan Fang,Junfeng Fang,Ningyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MemTrapBench基准，用于系统评估大语言模型记忆系统引发的认知陷阱（推理固化和信念扭曲），并提出了AdaptiveMem推理时方法以缓解这些陷阱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆基准主要评测信息是否正确提取、存储和检索，却忽略了检索到的记忆如何重塑模型推理并影响当前任务表现。即使记忆正确地记录且语义相关，也可能扭曲模型推理或信念，降低当前任务性能。

Method: 1) 提出MemTrapBench基准，覆盖两类认知陷阱：推理固化（Reasoning Fixation）和信念扭曲（Belief Distortion）；2) 提出AdaptiveMem，一种简单有效的推理时方法，通过指示LLM避免记忆陷阱来缓解问题。

Result: 在两个模型家族和五个代表性记忆框架上的实验表明，所有评估的记忆策略均低于无记忆设置，最强方法也遭受超过10%的性能下降；AdaptiveMem在MemTrapBench上缓解认知陷阱，同时在标准记忆基准上保持或改善各记忆框架的性能。

Conclusion: 记忆系统虽然能增强LLM的长期交互能力，但检索到的相关记忆可能反而损害当前任务推理，这是一个被低估的问题；AdaptiveMem提供了一种轻量级且有效的缓解方案。

Abstract: Memory has become a key component of large language models, enabling them to retain information and learn from long-term interactions. However, existing memory benchmarks mainly evaluate whether information is correctly extracted, stored, and retrieved, while largely overlooking how retrieved memories reshape model reasoning and affect performance on the current task. We identify memory-induced cognitive traps: even faithfully recorded and semantically relevant memories can distort model reasoning or beliefs and degrade current task performance. To systematically evaluate these failure modes, we introduce MemTrapBench, which covers two forms of cognitive traps: Reasoning Fixation and Belief Distortion. Experiments across two model families and five representative memory frameworks show that MemTrapBench is challenging: all evaluated memory strategies underperform the no-memory setting, with even the strongest methods suffering drops of more than 10%. To mitigate these cognitive traps, we propose AdaptiveMem, a simple yet effective inference-time method that instructs LLMs to avoid memory traps. AdaptiveMem mitigates cognitive traps on MemTrapBench while preserving or improving performance on standard memory benchmarks across diverse memory frameworks.

</details>


### [41] [ContractScrub: A benchmark for final review of legal contracts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20204)
*Yejin Bang,Kirsty Fielding,Brandan Oliver,Brian Birke,Nabeel Seedat,Andrew M. Bean*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次提出ContractScrub基准，用于评估LLM在合同"清洗"（合同最终审查）任务上的能力。研究发现前沿模型在此任务上表现不佳，仅一个模型达到0.75的宏平均召回率，尽管在相关通用基准上表现优异，揭示了当前模型的实践局限及领域特定基准的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合同清洗是法律工作中高频、日常且适合自动化的任务，涉及长上下文推理、一致性检查和命名实体识别等能力。尽管经济价值大且存在自动化潜力，但此前没有任何针对LLM执行合同清洗能力的正式评估。缺乏专门的基准来衡量模型在这一高价值领域的真实表现。

Method: 构建ContractScrub基准，包含由经验丰富的律师手工制作的合同，覆盖多种错误类别（如定义术语误用、错误引用、语言不一致等）。以此基准对前沿LLM进行系统评估，并对比其与相关通用基准的表现。

Result: 前沿模型在ContractScrub上表现令人惊讶地差，仅有一个模型达到0.75的宏平均召回率，尽管其在看似相关的通用基准上表现强劲。

Conclusion: 当前前沿LLM在合同清洗这类领域特定、高价值的实际任务上的表现存在明显局限，说明通用基准上的表现不足以预测真实世界领域任务的性能，强调需要针对性强、领域特定的基准来衡量实际影响。

Abstract: Legal work, with its heavy reliance on processing large amounts of text, is often considered one of the domains most exposed to the use of LLMs. Contract ``scrubbing,'' the final review of transactional agreements for errors and inconsistencies, is a particularly suitable task for automation, because it is routine, painstaking work requiring detailed attention to long documents. Scrubbing also seems to align naturally with the general capabilities expected of frontier LLMs around long-context reasoning, consistency checking, and named entity recognition (NER). Despite the economic value and potential for automation, no formal evaluations of LLMs performing contract scrubbing have been conducted. We introduce ContractScrub, the first benchmark designed to evaluate contract scrubbing capabilities, comprising contracts hand-crafted by experienced lawyers over diverse error categories such as misuse of defined terms, incorrect references, and inconsistent language. Frontier models perform surprisingly poorly with only one model reaching 0.75 macro average recall despite strong performance on seemingly related general benchmarks, demonstrating the practical limits of current models and the importance of narrowly targeted, domain-specific benchmarks for measuring real-world impact.

</details>


### [42] [Electronic Navigational Chart Change Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20218)
*Jacob Arndt,Abhishek Potnis,Alexandre Sorokine*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自动化分类电子海图（ENC）变更风险的方法，通过空间上下文编码器和ENC属性编码器将复杂的矢量数据变更转化为结构化表格格式，供梯度提升树等分类模型使用，在两个运营数据集（1,308对海图、超过10万次修改）上分别达到90%和94%的准确率，相比默认模型提高了5-7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水文办公室需要判断海图变更是对海事安全构成关键风险还是非关键风险。现有工作流依赖人工审查和验证，劳动密集、难以随海图更新量扩展，且存在分析师之间的不一致性。因此需要自动化方法来解决这一挑战。

Method: 提出一种自动分类ENC变更的方法。建立基线编码方案，将复杂矢量数据变更转化为结构化表格格式：包括空间上下文编码器（丰富周围地理特征的变更表示）和ENC属性编码器（表示修改对象细致的属性值描述）。使用调优的梯度提升树模型进行评估。

Result: 在两个不同的运营数据集上（共1,308对海图、超过100,000次独立海图修改），采用所提编码方案的调优梯度提升树分别达到90%和94%的准确率，相比使用无空间上下文和属性嵌入的默认超参数模型提高了5-7%。

Conclusion: 结果表明将机器学习集成到运营地理空间管道中是可行的，可改善ENC维护并增强海事安全。同时实验证明了简单位置和空间聚合方法的有效性，为评估更复杂的空间表示学习技术奠定了基础。

Abstract: Electronic Navigational Charts (ENCs) are geospatial vector datasets used in maritime navigation systems that represent hydrographic and navigational information such as depths, navigational aids, traffic schemes, and hazards. A major challenge for hydrographic offices is determining whether a given chart change poses a critical or non-critical risk to maritime safety. Existing workflows rely heavily on manual review and verification, which is labor-intensive, scales poorly with the volume of incoming chart updates, and introduces inter-analyst inconsistencies. To address this challenge, we propose a method for automated classification of ENC changes. We establish a baseline encoding scheme to translate complex vector data changes into a structured tabular format for classification models. The two crucial components of the encoding scheme include a spatial context encoder to enrich the change representations with surrounding geographic features, and an ENC attribute encoder to represent nuanced attribute-value descriptions of the modified objects. We evaluate the proposed approach across two distinct operational datasets, comprising 1,308 chart pairs containing over 100,000 individual chart modifications. Tuned gradient-boosted trees leveraging the proposed encoding schemes achieve accuracies of 90% and 94% on the two datasets, yielding a 5-7% improvement over default hyperparameterized models trained on encodings without spatial context and attribute embeddings. These results demonstrate the viability of integrating machine learning into operational geospatial pipelines to improve ENC maintenance and enhance maritime safety. Finally, our experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of simple location and spatial aggregation methods, providing a foundation for evaluating more sophisticated spatial representation learning techniques for this application.

</details>


### [43] [InsufficiencyBench: Evaluating LLM legal advice on underspecified user queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20220)
*Samuel J. Vincent,Daniel Calloway,Fangyi Yu,Andrew M. Bean,Nabeel Seedat*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出InsufficiencyBench，首个针对法律问答中查询侧信息不足问题的基准，包含202个测试项，涵盖8种缺失要素类别和3种结构性失效模式。评测10个前沿模型发现，其缺识缺失元素的F2分数最高仅0.46，中位召回率0.44，模型要么过度避险要么在臆造假设下直接作答，无法同时正确处理信息完整与不完整的查询。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有法律AI基准假设查询是完全明确的，但实际用户常省略对法律结果具有决定性影响的事实信息。缺乏一个专门评估模型能否识别法律查询中缺失关键信息、判断缺什么、并避免过早下结论的基准。

Method: 形式化了跨越三种结构性失效模式（switch切换、gating门控、fatal prerequisite致命前提）的八种缺失要素分类学，构建了202个基准测试项（58个基础查询、144个缺陷变体），覆盖6个法律领域和24个美国司法辖区，由执业律师进行标注。评测10个前沿模型。

Result: 没有模型在缺失要素识别上F2分数超过0.46，中位召回率为0.44。模型要么不加区分地含糊其辞，要么在臆造的假设下默默作答，且没有一个模型能同时对不完整查询进行识别和限定回答、又对完整查询直接作答。

Conclusion: 当前前沿法律AI系统在识别查询侧信息不足方面存在显著局限，需要改进模型对缺失关键法律要素的辨识能力与恰当的限缩回答行为。

Abstract: Legal AI systems are increasingly used to answer legal questions, yet existing benchmarks assume queries arrive fully specified. In practice, users omit facts that materially determine the legal outcome. We introduce InsufficiencyBench, the first legal benchmark targeting query-side insufficiency: whether a model recognizes when a query lacks legally material information, identifies what is missing, and refrains from premature conclusions. We formalize a taxonomy of eight canonical missing-element categories across three structural failure modes---switch, gating, and fatal prerequisite--- and construct 202 benchmark items (58 base queries, 144 deficient variants) spanning six legal domains and 24 US jurisdictions and annotated by practising attorneys. Evaluating ten frontier models, we find that no model exceeds F2 = 0.46 on missing-element identification and that the median recall is 0.44. Models either hedge indiscriminately or answer silently under fabricated presumptions. No model both identifies and qualifies responses to deficient queries while directly addressing complete ones.

</details>

*Yu Chen,Ting Lei,Yaoyi Li,Jia Cai,Zhecen Wu,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RuleMaze基准测试，评估多模态大语言模型在自然语言规则约束下进行视觉空间规划的能力；同时提出Language-Logic-Function Hybridization自动生成规则，以及Disentangled Multimodal Planning (DMP)方法分离感知、执行与规则验证，提升规则遵循能力与系统化泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）结合了语言推理与视觉感知，但其在显式或未见过的规则约束下进行视觉空间规划的能力尚未被充分探索。现有工作缺乏可控的基准来隔离测试该类模型在"感知-规则理解-约束规划"联合任务上的表现。

Method: (1) 提出RuleMaze基准：MLLMs需在迷宫环境中遵循复杂度可变的自然语言规则进行导航；(2) 提出Language-Logic-Function Hybridization：自动生成自然语言规则并转化为逻辑表示与可执行验证器，无需手工规则工程；(3) 提出Disentangled Multimodal Planning (DMP)：通过可解释的推理原语分离感知、执行与规则验证三个环节，支持向更复杂与未见规则的系统化泛化，并提供透明的中间规划轨迹。

Result: 实验表明，与端到端文本规划基线相比，DMP方法显著提升了规则遵循度与规划成功率。

Conclusion: RuleMaze为研究MLLMs中基于规则、可解释的落地空间规划建立了原则性基准；DMP通过组件解耦实现了更好的规则遵循与泛化能力，代码已开源。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) combine linguistic reasoning with visual perception, yet their ability to perform visual spatial planning under explicit or previously unseen rule constraints remains underexplored. This setting requires models to jointly understand spatial layouts, interpret natural-language rules, and plan valid actions accordingly. To address this gap, we introduce RuleMaze, a controllable benchmark in which MLLMs must navigate mazes while obeying natural-language rules of varying complexity. RuleMaze isolates rule-compliant spatial planning by requiring accurate perception, rule interpretation, and constrained action planning. To enable scalable and systematic rule construction, we propose Language-Logic-Function Hybridization, which automatically generates natural-language rules and translates them into logical representations and executable validators, eliminating manual rule engineering. To improve rule following and generalization, we introduce Disentangled Multimodal Planning (DMP), which separates perception, execution, and rule verification through interpretable reasoning primitives. By disentangling these components, DMP facilitates systematic generalization to more complex and previously unseen rules, while providing transparent intermediate planning traces. Experiments demonstrate that DMP substantially improves rule compliance and planning success compared to end-to-end textual planning baselines. Overall, RuleMaze establishes a principled benchmark for studying grounded and interpretable rule-based spatial planning in MLLMs. Code is available at https://github.com/oceanflowlab/RuleMaze.

</details>


### [45] [Manifold Drift in Flow Preference Optimization: A Root Cause of Reward Hacking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20011)
*Yansen Han,Shengyi Liao,Yuanxing Zhang,Pengfei Wan,Tao Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 ThermoDPO，一种温度控制的偏好优化目标，用于解决流匹配中偏好更新导致的流形漂移（manifold drift）问题。方法通过锚定偏好样本控制模型停留在预训练数据流形上，并引入加权变体 ThermoDPO-weighted 应对低温下的信号衰减。在玩具基准上 StrictScore 达 0.899（对比 FlowDPO 的 0.629），在 SD3.5-M 上 OCR 提升 47.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏好优化是生成模型的标准对齐方法，但将其扩展到连续时间动力学（流匹配）时存在根本性问题：奖励驱动的更新会修改传输轨迹却缺乏对预训练数据流形的约束，导致终端样本偏离预训练支持范围。作者将此失败模式形式化为"流形漂移"。

Method: 作者首先从理论上证明：最优流匹配可恢复终端数据分布，而偏好更新在诱导终端位移具有非零法向分量时会偏离预训练流形。基于此，提出 ThermoDPO——一个温度控制的目标函数，将成对偏好优化锚定在偏好样本上。该目标在温度区间内连接拒绝采样微调（RFT）和 FlowDPO，并控制基于逐点重建的流形距离代理。针对低温下信号衰减，进一步提出加权变体 ThermoDPO-weighted。

Result: 在主要玩具基准上，ThermoDPO-weighted 达到 StrictScore 0.899，优于 FlowDPO 的 0.629 和 FlowDPO+RFT 的 0.857。在 SD3.5-M 模型上（CFG=4.5），OCR 指标提升 47.5%，四项指标平均提升 16.0%。

Conclusion: ThermoDPO 通过温度控制的锚定机制有效缓解了流匹配中的流形漂移问题，在玩具基准和真实模型上均取得显著改进，表明该方法是流匹配偏好优化的有效方案。

Abstract: Preference optimization is a standard alignment method for generative models, yet extending it to continuous-time dynamics remains non-trivial. In flow matching, reward-driven updates modify transport trajectories without an inherent constraint to the pretrained data manifold and can move terminal samples off the pretrained support. We formalize this failure mode as manifold drift. Theoretically, we show that optimal flow matching recovers the terminal data distribution, whereas a preference update leaves the pretrained manifold whenever its induced terminal displacement has a nonzero normal component. As a remedy, we propose ThermoDPO, a temperature-controlled objective that anchors pairwise preference optimization on preferred samples. Across temperature regimes, this objective connects rejection sampling fine-tuning and FlowDPO and controls a pointwise reconstruction-based surrogate for manifold distance. To counteract diminished signals at low temperatures, we further introduce a weighted variant, ThermoDPO-weighted. On the main toy benchmark, ThermoDPO-weighted attains a StrictScore of 0.899, compared with 0.629 for FlowDPO and 0.857 for FlowDPO+RFT. On SD3.5-M at CFG = 4.5, it improves OCR by 47.5% and the average of four metrics by 16.0%.

</details>


### [46] [Learning When to Think: Adaptive Reasoning for Test-Time Compute Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20256)
*Gijs Kassenaar,Zhao Yang,Vincent François-Lavet*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究推理语言模型能否自适应地分配推理预算。模型通过在回复的第一个token选择三种模式（NoThink/Short/Long）来决定推理强度，该选择通过GRPO强化学习原生学习，无需独立路由器。在1.5B蒸馏模型上，三种模式自然涌现而不坍缩，短模式比长模式更准确（说明路由器按难度分类有效）。MATH500上准确率几乎不变（0.782 vs 0.796），但平均响应长度从4,796降至2,811个token（减少41%），且能零训练迁移到其他基准（GSM8K上节省76% token且准确率更高）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理语言模型在强化学习训练下使用固定token预算进行推理，而非自适应的token预算，这会导致在简单问题上过度计算、在困难问题上计算不足。作者希望探究模型能否自行学习分配推理强度，让推理代价与问题难度相匹配。

Method: 模型的回复以第一个token显式选择三种模式之一：NoThink（快速回答）、Short（简要推理）、Long（扩展推理）。模式选择在GRPO框架内端到端学习，无需独立的路由器模块。通过设计一个shape奖励来使每种模式在不同响应长度下都有价值，同时对每种模式设置硬性token上限以保持模式间的区分度。

Result: 在1.5B蒸馏模型上训练于MATH数据集：三种模式自然涌现且未坍缩为单一选择；短模式比长模式更准确，证明路由器按问题难度而非随机分类。三颗种子平均结果：MATH500准确率接近基线（0.782 vs 0.796），平均响应长度从4,796降至2,811个token（41%缩减）。无需重新训练即可迁移至其他基准，在GSM8K上节省76% token且在同响应长度下准确率高于基线。

Conclusion: 作者构建了一个能够为每个问题自适应选择推理强度的推理模型，在保持准确率几乎不变的同时大幅降低计算开销，且该能力具有跨基准的泛化性。这证明推理模型可以通过自学习的方式实现按问题难度的自适应计算分配。

Abstract: Reasoning language models trained with reinforcement learning typically operate under a fixed token budget rather than an explicitly adaptive one, which can lead to over-computation on easy problems and insufficient computation on difficult ones. We study whether a model can learn to allocate its own reasoning effort by choosing, as the first token of its response, one of three modes: \textsc{NoThink} (answer as quickly as possible), \textsc{Short} (brief reasoning), or \textsc{Long} (extended reasoning). The choice is learned inside Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with no separate router, through a shaped reward that makes each mode worthwhile at a different response length, together with hard per-mode token caps that keep the modes distinct. On a 1.5B distilled model trained on MATH, the three modes emerge without collapsing to a single choice, and the brief modes end up more accurate than \textsc{Long}, which shows that the router sorts problems by difficulty rather than at random. Averaged over three seeds, the resulting policy stays close to the base model's accuracy on the held-out MATH500 ($0.782$ vs.\ $0.796$) while cutting the mean response length from $4{,}796$ to $2{,}811$ tokens (a $41\%$ reduction). Interestingly, it also transfers to other benchmarks without retraining, with the largest savings where problems are easier, with for instance 76\% token reduction on GSM8K and at higher accuracy than the baselines at similar response length. In short, we build a reasoning model that adaptively chooses how much to reason for each problem.

</details>


### [47] [Catching the Rug: Early Prediction of Fraudulent Memecoins on Solana via Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20271)
*Jianghai Li,Pavel Kuznetsov,Yury Yanovich,Konstantin Nott-Whaley,Igor Vodolazov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究首次在Solana生态中开展大规模Rug Pull早期检测，构建了7个月640万代币的数据集，发现绝大多数memecoin在发布一小时内即表现出Rug Pull特征。利用仅前5分钟的交易数据，经典机器学习模型（尤其XGBoost）即可实现稳健的检测性能，且跨平台（PumpFun和Raydium）多源数据融合可显著缓解域偏移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Solana已成为memecoin交易量和代币数量的领先区块链，但其Rug Pull欺诈风险高且与以太坊的欺诈机制不同（以太坊多利用智能合约后门，Solana则主要依赖流动性操纵和社会动态）。此前研究集中于以太坊，缺乏对Solana生态大规模Rug Pull早期检测的研究。

Method: 组装了包含640万代币、覆盖7个月时间的Solana生态大型数据集；采用仅基于前5分钟交易数据的经典机器学习模型（重点为XGBoost梯度提升）；评估了PumpFun与Raydium之间的跨平台泛化能力，并采用多源数据融合来缓解域偏移。

Result: 经典机器学习模型（特别是XGBoost）仅凭前5分钟交易数据即可实现稳健的Rug Pull检测性能；绝大多数memecoin在发布一小时内即表现出Rug Pull特征；多源数据融合显著缓解了跨平台域偏移并提高了检测可靠性。

Conclusion: 该研究推进了对高吞吐量链上DeFi欺诈的理解，为保护投资者提供了实用的检测框架，并证明了在缺乏代码级特征的情况下仍能实现有效的早期Rug Pull检测。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of memecoins on blockchain platforms has increased the risk of fraudulent activities, particularly rug pulls. While previous studies have focused on Ethereum-based tokens, this paper shifts the spotlight to Solana, the leading blockchain for memecoins by trading volume and token count. Unlike Ethereum, where rug pulls often exploit smart contract backdoors, Solana memecoin rug pulls are predominantly driven by liquidity manipulation and social dynamics. This research pioneers large-scale rug pull early detection in the Solana ecosystem by assembling a dataset of 6.4 million tokens over 7 months. Market analysis reveals that a vast majority of these memecoins exhibit rug pull characteristics within one hour of launch, highlighting the urgency of short-horizon prediction. Despite the absence of code-level features, we demonstrate that classic machine learning models, particularly Gradient Boosting (XGBoost), achieve robust performance in detecting potential rug pulls using only the first 5 minutes of trading data. Furthermore, we evaluate cross-platform generalization between PumpFun and Raydium, revealing that multi-source data fusion significantly mitigates domain shift and improves detection reliability. This study advances the understanding of DeFi fraud on high-throughput chains and provides a practical framework for protecting investors.

</details>


### [48] [Break It Down, Pass It On: Cross-Task Skill Transfer in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20274)
*Yiyang Feng,Biddut Sarker Bijoy,Niranjan Balasubramanian,Jiawei Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对LLM代理技能诱导方式（任务级vs子任务级，文本vs代码格式）如何影响技能跨任务迁移进行了全面的受控研究，发现子任务级技能和文本格式技能迁移效果更好，并提出了一种只需技能和任务描述、无需任务执行的"技能效用分数"作为实用诊断工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理可从已完成任务中诱导技能并复用以增强能力，但实际中诱导的技能可能迁移不可靠甚至伤害代理。技能能否可靠跨任务迁移的关键条件尚不明确，需要系统研究。

Method: 沿两个现有方法差异的轴进行受控对比：任务级vs子任务级技能诱导，文本vs代码技能格式。通过综合受控实验分析，并引入两个互补属性（特异性与抽象性）来检验诱导技能，提出二者联合作用的技能效用分数作为预测指标。

Result: 任务级技能大多将代理性能降低到无记忆基线以下，而子任务级技能平均将其提升到基线以上；文本技能比代码技能迁移更好。特异性或抽象性单一属性均不能预测任务成功，但二者的联合效应（技能效用分数）能一致地预测技能迁移时的任务成功率，且子任务级与文本技能得分更高。

Conclusion: 技能效用分数仅需技能和任务描述而无需任务执行，可在任何新任务运行前作为技能记忆的实用诊断指标，为LLM代理的技能诱导与记忆管理提供指导。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents can induce skills from completed tasks and reuse them later to grow more capable with experience. In practice, induced skills may transfer unreliably and can even harm the agent that retrieves them. When agent-induced skills transfer reliably across tasks remains an open question. We conduct a comprehensive and controlled study of how the way skills are induced shapes their transfer across tasks. Specifically, we compare task-level with subtask-level skill induction and text with code skill formats, the two axes along which existing methods differ. Task-level skills mostly reduce the agent's performance below its no-memory baseline while subtask-level skills raise it above on average, and text skills transfer better than code skills. To further understand our findings, we examine two complementary properties of the induced skills: specificity, which measures how closely a skill matches real tasks, and abstractness, which measures how evenly its relevance spreads across tasks. Neither property alone predicts task success, but their combined effect does, which we propose as a skill utility score. The score correlates consistently with task success when skills are transferred, and subtask-level and text skills score higher. Computing skill utility only needs the skills and task descriptions but not any task execution, so our score serves as a practical diagnostic of a skill memory before any new task runs.

</details>


### [49] [Phantom Gains: Auditing Self-Improvement Against a Measured Null](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20290)
*Cheng Xu,Nan Yan,Liming Chen,M-Tahar Kechadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出，评估语言模型自我改进时常用"逐问题得失"（transition-level）指标来替代平均准确率，但这些指标基于两次有噪估计的差分，极易受测量伪影影响。作者对Qwen3-8B在秩32 LoRA自训练三轮设置下进行审计，并以相同流水线处理冻结对照组，发现七类测量失败——每一项在缺少对照时都会反转报告的结论。其中多项是标准做法：单次贪婪解码构建的账本会在未训练模型上制造能力变化（绝大部分是推理批处理伪影）；"扩展统计量"给未训练模型分配0.280的比率。自然阈值修复无法在复制中存续。作者提出基于合并基线的逐问题精确检验，在错误发现率控制下，对所有保留复制无检出，且对多重检验规则、错误率与池大小不敏感。应用于流水匹配的多臂实验后，发现外部蒸馏提高了基模型很少触及的问题，而三种自训练形式没有；回归检验拒绝了该不对称仅是蒸馏整体增益更大的副产品（p<1e-8）。在基模型从未触及的极小问题集上证据不足，而自训练在基线已解决问题上的损坏率远超实测底部。因此，transition-level审计需要对每个统计量都建立单独测量的零模型——这些零模型无需新实验，来自多臂研究已有的基线复制，但大多数研究所拥有的复制数量不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型自我改进的评估正从平均准确率转向逐问题的"获得/丢失"（transition-level）追踪，但这些指标通过对两个有噪估计做差得到，极易被测量伪影误导。作者旨在系统审计并量化这些伪影，并提供可靠的分析方法。

Method: 以秩32 LoRA在Qwen3-8B上做三轮自训练，将冻结对照通过完全相同的流水线处理以作为控制。识别七类测量失败；提出以合并基线的逐问题精确检验（在FDR控制下）替代自然阈值修复；在多臂（外部蒸馏与三种自训练）阶梯实验中匹配数据流、规模与评估，用回归检验排除混淆。

Result: 七类测量失败每一项在缺少对照时都会反转结论；单次贪婪解码与批处理伪影可在未训练模型上产生能力变化，扩展统计量给未训练模型0.280的比率；自然阈值修复复制失败。新提出的逐问题精确检验在所有保留复制上无检出，且对检验规则、错误率和池大小稳健。外部蒸馏能改善基模型少涉及的问题，三种自训练不能，回归显示该不对称非整体增益更大所致（p<1e-8）；在基模型从未触及的小子集上证据不足，自训练在基线已解决问题上的损坏率显著超过实测底部。

Conclusion: Transition-level审计要求对每个报告统计量都建立单独的、实验内测量的零假设分布，这些零假设可免费取自多臂研究已有的基线复制；但大多数研究所拥有的基线复制数量不足。没有对照的逐问题得失分析结论不可信。

Abstract: Whether a language model has improved itself is increasingly judged not by mean accuracy but by which individual problems it gains and loses. Tracking these transitions means differencing two noisy estimates, leaving them vulnerable to measurement artifacts. Auditing three rounds of rank-$32$ LoRA self-training on Qwen3-8B against a frozen control pushed through the identical pipeline, we identify seven measurement failures, each of which inverts a reported finding when its control is absent. Several are standard practice. A ledger built on a single greedy decode manufactures capability changes on an untrained model, largely an artifact of inference batching; the expansion statistic separating acquisition from sharpening assigns that same model a rate of $0.280$. The natural threshold repair does not survive replication: estimated across the frozen comparisons such a design already contains, its null stays non-zero. We replace it with a per-problem exact test against a pooled baseline under false-discovery-rate control, which detects nothing on any held-out replicate and is unchanged under the multiple-testing rule, error rate and pool size. Applied to a ladder of arms matched in stream, volume and evaluation, the audit finds that external distillation improves problems the base model rarely reaches while three forms of self-training do not; a regression rejects this asymmetry as a by-product of distillation's larger overall gain ($p < 10^{-8}$). On the far smaller set of problems the base model never reaches, the evidence is inconclusive, while self-training corrupts problems solved at baseline at rates well above the measured floor. Transition-level auditing therefore requires a separately measured null for every statistic it reports: nulls that cost no new experiments, built from baseline replicates a multi-arm study already owns, though not from as few as most possess.

</details>


### [50] [MidTool: Mid-training Data Synthesis for Agentic Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20314)
*Fengqing Jiang,Yite Wang,Boyi Liu,Zhaoyang Wang,Canwen Xu,Zhewei Yao,Radha Poovendran,Yuxiong He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MidTool，一个针对通用工具使用（agentic tool use）能力的开源语料构建与中训练（mid-training）流程。MidTool整合大规模网页、PDF、代码数据以及来自真实工具API、MCP技能和文档驱动工作流的合成监督信号，旨在教会模型识别工具affordance、从上下文中构建参数、组合工具调用工作流以及从不完整信息中恢复。作者在Qwen3-4B-Base和Qwen3-8B-Base上进行中训练，后续叠加SFT和RL后训练，在BFCL、tau2-Bench和MCP Universe上均稳定优于基线，表明通用工具使用能力同样需要专门的中训练而非完全依赖后训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来中训练（mid-training）被认为是塑造大语言模型能力的关键阶段，已有工作证明其能增强数学、科学等推理密集型能力以及软件工程场景中的agentic能力。但通用工具使用这一同样重要的agentic能力尚未被充分探索。作者希望验证通用工具使用是否也能从中训练中获益。

Method: 提出MidTool开源语料构建流水线，融合大规模网页、PDF与代码数据，以及从真实工具API、MCP技能和文档驱动工作流中合成的监督数据。设计上教模型四类能力：识别工具affordance、从上下文构建参数、组合工具调用工作流、从不完整信息中恢复。在Qwen3-4B-Base和Qwen3-8B-Base上进行中训练，随后分别进行SFT和RL后训练。

Result: 在BFCL、tau2-Bench和MCP Universe三个基准上，MidTool-Mix在SFT和RL两种后训练设置下均一致地提升了下游性能，优于基线。

Conclusion: 通用工具使用与其他重要的大语言模型能力一样，应当获得专门的中训练阶段支持，而非完全留给后训练阶段处理。

Abstract: Mid-training is increasingly recognized as a critical stage for shaping the capabilities of large language models. Recent work has shown that targeted mid-training can strengthen reasoning-intensive abilities such as math and science, and can also improve agentic capabilities in software-engineering settings. In this work, we study the parallel but less explored agentic capability: general tool use. We present MidTool, an open corpus construction pipeline for agentic tool-use mid-training that combines large-scale web, PDF, and code data with synthesized supervision from real-world tool APIs, MCP skills, and document-grounded workflows. MidTool is designed to teach models how to recognize tool affordances, ground arguments from context, compose tool call workflow, and recover from incomplete information. We mid-train Qwen3-4B-Base and Qwen3-8B-Base on MidTool-Mix, and then apply follow-up post-training with both supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning. Compared with baselines, MidTool-Mix consistently improves downstream performance under both SFT and RL on BFCL, tau2-Bench, and MCP Universe. These results suggest that general tool use, like other important LLM capabilities, benefits from dedicated mid-training rather than being left entirely to post-training.

</details>


### [51] [Pandora's AI Model Routing Box: Efficient Allocation with Costly Value Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20316)
*Adam Fisch,Shubhendu Trivedi,Fantine Huot,William W. Cohen,Michael Kaisers,Mirella Lapata,Kate Larson,Jacob Eisenstein*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Pandora's Router和Pandora's Bidder两种路由策略，将异构AI系统中"评估专科模型价值"的成本-收益权衡建模为Pandora's Box问题。在高斯信号模型下推导出封闭形式的信息价值表达式，在集中式和去中心化场景中决定是否值得付费细化价值估计。实验表明该方法能以远低于穷举估计的查询成本达到同等路由质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构AI系统由多个模型、架构或推理设置组成，需要将查询路由到最有效且成本最低的专家。但路由本身需要估计每位专家的预期回报，而价值估计存在成本：廉价估计器（如基于嵌入的预测器）快速但有噪声，昂贵估计器（如访问检索结果或部分推理轨迹的微调模型）准确但开销大。现有方法未能系统形式化这种权衡。

Method: 将价值估计的成本-收益权衡形式化为Pandora's Box问题（经典的有代价检查的最优搜索问题）。在高斯信号模型假设下，推导出封闭形式的信息价值表达式，用于决定对每个专家和输入是否值得细化价值估计。提出两种策略：(1) Pandora's Router——集中式策略；(2) Pandora's Bidder——去中心化策略，专家独立决定是否投资于自我评估后再接受报价认领查询。

Result: 在三个领域（标准多LLM基准、检索增强专家、可变推理时推理的LLM）上的实验表明：Pandora's Router能以匹配穷举估计的路由质量，同时大幅减少对昂贵估计器的查询频率。在去中心化场景中，信息价值推理在竞争估计准确时能提高分配效率；但当竞争估计有噪声时，会以牺牲他人效用为代价增加策略性专家的效用。

Conclusion: 本文从信息价值理论角度为异构AI系统的路由问题提供了新的理论框架，证明了"何时值得付费进行更精细的专家评估"这一问题具有可计算的最优解，并在集中式和去中心化两种场景下均验证了有效性。同时也揭示了去中心化场景中噪声估计可能导致的策略性博弈问题。

Abstract: Heterogeneous AI systems composed of multiple models, architectures, harnesses, or inference-time settings can improve quality and efficiency by routing queries to the specialist who can answer most effectively at the lowest cost. Routing requires estimating each specialist's expected return, but this value estimation has a cost. Cheap estimators (e.g., embedding-based predictors) are fast but noisy, while accurate estimators (e.g., fine-tuned models with access to retrieval results or partial reasoning traces) are expensive. We formalize this tradeoff as an instance of Pandora's Box, the classical problem of optimal search with costly inspection. Under a Gaussian signal model, the resulting policies have closed-form value-of-information expressions that determine, for each specialist and input, whether refining the value estimate is worth its cost. We call the centralized policy Pandora's Router. We extend this to a decentralized setting, Pandora's Bidder, where specialists independently decide whether to invest in self-assessment before accepting an offered price to claim a query. Experiments across three domains---a standard multi-LLM benchmark, retrieval-augmented specialists, and LLMs with variable inference-time reasoning---show that Pandora's Router matches the routing quality of exhaustive estimation, while querying the expensive estimator far less often. In the decentralized setting, value-of-information reasoning improves allocative efficiency when competing estimates are accurate; when competing estimates are noisy, however, it can increase the strategic specialist's utility at the expense of others.

</details>


### [52] [AI4AI-Bench: Benchmarking LLM Agents in Algorithmic Design for Recursive Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20318)
*Yizhe Chi,Wenyi Li,Deyao Hong,Xiaoqiu Wang,Mingju Gao,Kaisen Yang,Bingxiang He,Youjie Zheng,Calvin Xiao,Qinhuai Na*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AI4AI-Bench，一个包含10个冻结研究仓库、覆盖10种训练算法家族的基准测试，用于衡量AI系统递归自我改进（RSI）的能力——即能否重新设计训练算法本身。在29个配置、6个系统的跨任务测试中，平均得分仅0.166，最强系统达到0.250（不足最优距离的五分之一）。分析显示多数系统根本不改变模型学习方式，而改变者平均得分0.226 vs 0.126；更多推理努力能将改变训练算法的提交从8%提升到64%，平均分从0.094提升到0.196。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归自我改进（RSI）的核心问题在于AI系统能否改进产生AI系统的训练算法本身，但现有基准测试均无法隔离和衡量这一能力——现有测试要么靠收集数据获胜，要么靠调超参数取胜，没有区分"改变运行方式"和"改变模型学习方式"。

Method: 构建AI4AI-Bench：10个冻结的研究仓库，覆盖10种训练算法家族。每个任务中，智能体有4小时在B300上重写训练算法，其代码随后从头重新运行最多12小时，由对智能体隐藏的固定评估器评分，并与仓库原始算法在相同流程下比较。由于10个指标不可公度，每个任务映射到统一尺度（0为无信息模型，0.1为仓库自带算法，1.0为任务最优）。

Result: 6个系统在29个配置下跨越所有10个任务的平均得分0.166，最强系统达到0.250。多数提交从未改变模型的学习方式；少数确实改变者平均0.226，其余为0.126。增加推理努力使改变训练算法的提交比例从8%升至64%，平均分从0.094升至0.196。

Conclusion: 即使最强的AI系统在重新设计训练算法方面也只闭合了最优距离的不到五分之一，表明RSI在当前系统能力下远未实现。论文发布任务套件、评估器和所有评分提交，以便随着系统演进可重复测量。

Abstract: Recursive self-improvement (RSI) asks whether an AI system can improve the process that produces AI systems, so that the next system inherits the improvement. That process is the training algorithm: a better objective or update rule improves the compute\mbox{-}capability exchange rate for every subsequent run, including the one that produces the next agent. Whether RSI is feasible therefore turns on whether an agent can design training algorithms. No benchmark isolates that ability: existing suites are won by collecting data or by tuning hyperparameters, and none tells a change to how a run is executed apart from a change to how the model learns. We present AI4AI\mbox{-}Bench, 10 frozen research repositories spanning 10 training algorithm families. In each task, an agent has 4 hours on one B300 to rewrite the training algorithm; its code is then rerun from scratch for up to 12 hours and scored by a fixed evaluator hidden from the agent, against the repository's original algorithm under the same procedure. Because the 10 metrics are incommensurable, every task is mapped onto one scale on which $0$ is an uninformative model, $0.1$ is the algorithm the repository ships, and $1.0$ is the task optimum. Across 29 configurations of 6 systems on all 10 tasks the mean score is $0.166$, and the best system reaches $0.250$: even the strongest closes under a fifth of the distance between the algorithm that was already there and the optimum. The submissions show where that distance went: most never change how the model learns at all, and the minority that do average $0.226$ against $0.126$ for the rest. More reasoning effort mostly buys the willingness to go there, taking that minority from $8\%$ of submissions to $64\%$ and the mean score from $0.094$ to $0.196$. We release the task suite, the evaluators and every scored submission, so that the measurement can be repeated as these systems change.

</details>


### [53] [An Agentic Approach for Active Data Collection, Travel Behavior Modeling, and Weather-Sensitive Demand Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20320)
*Narges Ahmadi,Yubo Jiao,Jônatas Augusto Manzolli,Jiangbo Yu,Luis Miranda-Moreno*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个三智能体工作流，整合对话式数据收集、结构化数据处理和行为预测。通过聊天机器人的图像增强陈述偏好调查收集了学生通勤者在五种天气场景下的454个模式选择观测数据，评价了多类别日志it模型、机器学习基准以及9个本地部署的大语言模型（含视觉配置）。随机森林达到69.6%五类准确率，最佳纯文本零样本LLM达69.9%，而最佳视觉配置达到71.5%五类准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 出行行为研究日益将数字数据收集与预测建模相结合，但这两个阶段通常被分开开发和评估，缺乏一个统一的、可审计的多智能体工作流来协调数据收集、数据处理和行为预测各环节。

Method: 采用三智能体工作流：(1) 聊天机器人管理的图像增强陈述偏好调查收集数据；(2) 结构化数据处理与多元logit模型分析天气相关关联；(3) 用逻辑回归和随机森林作为机器学习基准，同时评估9个参数规模从2亿到350亿的本地部署LLM，在四种零样本提示情境下以及人格设定、少样本和视觉配置下进行预测。研究还比较了专家框架与角色扮演框架，以及有无习惯性出行信息和人格信息的多种条件。

Result: 454个受访者-场景观测。随机森林达到69.6%五类准确率；最佳纯文本零样本LLM在无需任务特定拟合的情况下达到69.9%；最优视觉配置（使用与受访者相同的天气图像）达到71.5%五类准确率。习惯性出行信息带来最一致的提升，专家框架通常优于角色扮演，人格信息在缺乏习惯性出行信息时最有用，少样本提示对小部分模型有改进且收益在少量样本后趋于稳定。

Conclusion: 研究表明对话式调查、结构化数据处理、传统行为建模、机器学习和多模态LLM预测可以在一个可审计的多智能体工作流内协调运作，视觉上下文可能为部分模型提供额外的预测信息，LLM在零样本条件下即可与传统机器学习基准相匹敌。

Abstract: Travel behavior research increasingly combines digital data collection with predictive modeling, yet these stages are often developed and evaluated separately. This study proposes a three-agent workflow integrating conversational data collection, structured data processing, and behavioral prediction. A chatbot-administered, image-augmented stated-preference survey collected mode choices from student commuters across five predefined weather scenarios, yielding 454 respondent-scenario observations. Weather-related associations were analyzed using a multinomial logit model, while logistic regression and random forest provided machine-learning benchmarks. Nine locally deployed large language models (LLMs), ranging from 2 to 35 billion parameters, were evaluated across four zero-shot prompt-and-context conditions and extended through persona, few-shot, and vision-based configurations. Random forest achieved 69.6% five-class accuracy, while the best text-only zero-shot LLM reached 69.9% without task-specific fitting. Habitual travel information produced the most consistent gains, Expert framing generally outperformed Role-Play, and persona information was most useful when habitual travel information was unavailable. Few-shot prompting improved prediction for several models, with gains stabilizing after a small number of examples. Using the same weather images shown to respondents, the best vision-based configuration reached 71.5% five-class accuracy, indicating that visual context may provide additional predictive information for selected models. Overall, the study shows how conversational surveys, structured data processing, conventional behavioral modeling, machine learning, and multimodal LLM prediction can be coordinated within an auditable multi-agent workflow.

</details>

### [54] [Clustering and Token Denoising for Faster and More Robust VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19285)
*Baptiste Rossigneux,Inna Kucher,Vincent Lorrain,Emmanuel Casseau*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ClustRS是一个无需训练的、两部分的token剪枝算法，用于视觉语言模型（如LLaVA）的稳健token剪枝。它通过注意力加权聚类选择代表性token，并通过残差收缩进行一步去噪，在极端的token缩减和噪声条件下显著优于现有的基于注意力和多样性的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM中处理数百个视觉token的计算负担使得边缘设备部署困难。现有的token剪枝技术大多需要重新训练，缺乏对架构变化的适应性，因此需要一种无需训练的、对噪声稳健的token剪枝方法。

Method: ClustRS由两部分组成：(1) 注意力加权的聚类算法，从每个语义簇中选择代表性token；(2) 残差收缩（Residual Shrinkage）步骤，对选出的token进行一步去噪处理。整个方法无需训练，仅需轻量级计算。

Result: 在ScienceQA-IMG和MM-VET基准上，ClustRS在极端噪声和token条件下（token缩减97%至16个）相比基于注意力和多样性的方法提升高达20%。在LLaVA-OneVision上，该方法在温和噪声条件下使用不到三分之一的token即可匹配基线性能。

Conclusion: ClustRS证明了一种简单而强大的替代方案，超越了仅基于分数或仅基于多样性的剪枝规则，为计算高效且噪声鲁棒的VLM部署铺平了道路。

Abstract: Recent Visual-Language Models (VLMs) have enhanced the capabilities of pre-trained LLMs by adding vision tokens alongside text, with approaches like LLaVA showing impressive results. However, the computational burden of processing up to 576 or 729 visual tokens makes edge deployment challenging. While various token pruning techniques require retraining, some are training-free and thus can easily adapt to architecture changes. We introduce ClustRS, a two-part, training-free algorithm for robust token pruning. Its first component is an attention-weighted, clustering algorithm that selects representative tokens from each semantic cluster. The second component, Residual Shrinkage, is a one-pass denoising step on the selected tokens. These training-free lightweight steps make LLaVA ready for real-world data, improving robustness to a wide range of image-noise types and intensities. Experimental results on the ScienceQA-IMG and MM-VET benchmarks show our method outperforms attention- and diversity-based methods by up to 20\% under extreme noise and token conditions (reducing tokens by 97\%, down to 16 tokens) on LLaVA 1.5 7b and achieves exceptional results on LLaVA-OneVision, where we match baseline performance with fewer than one-third of their tokens under mild noise conditions. Our study demonstrates a simple yet powerful alternative to both score-only and diversity-only pruning rules, paving the way for compute-efficient and noise-resilient VLM deployment.

</details>


### [55] [SceneGTMM: A Conformal Mapping-based Scene-Aware Transferable GNN-Transformer Dual-Graph Interaction Framework for Map Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19298)
*Yongliang Zhang,Feng Song,Ji Chen,Lishuai Guo,Yong Deng,Yue Zheng,Tianyi Liu,Zhixiong Chen,Qixin Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SceneGTMM，一种基于共形映射场景相对策略的可迁移GNN-Transformer双图交互地图匹配框架，通过构建以轨迹为中心的局部坐标系实现跨区域迁移，结合GNN局部拓扑约束、Transformer全局时序依赖以及CRF结构化预测，在16-50米定位误差下达到超过80%的准确率，较HMM提升5.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地图匹配方法在局部与全局信息融合、动态路网自适应以及可解释性方面存在不足，且严重依赖黑盒模型。针对噪声鲁棒性差、跨区域迁移能力弱和可解释性缺乏的问题，需要一种既能适应动态路网变化又能跨区域泛化的高精度匹配框架。

Method: 方法包含三大核心模块：1) 基于共形映射的场景相对策略，构建以轨迹为中心的局部坐标系，减少对训练路网的依赖；2) GNN-Transformer双图交互架构，GNN建模路网图捕获局部拓扑约束，Transformer建模轨迹图捕获全局时序依赖，通过跨图注意力实现噪声抑制与语义对齐；3) CRF增强的结构化预测，融合Transformer全局上下文与CRF拓扑转移约束提升路径连通性与鲁棒性。

Result: 在16-50米定位误差的多源轨迹上达到超过80%的准确率，较HMM提升5.3%；在跨城市迁移场景中优于MTrajRec、GraphMM和TMM基线方法，并通过注意力与相对坐标可视化增强了模型可解释性。

Conclusion: SceneGTMM为高精度、可迁移的地图匹配提供了新范式，对实时交通感知和自动驾驶路径规划具有重要应用价值，其场景相对策略有效解决了跨区域迁移和动态路网更新的核心难题。

Abstract: Map matching is a key technology connecting positioning data with high precision road networks, but it faces challenges in noise robustness, cross regional transfer, and interpretability. To addr ess the limitations of existing methods in local global fusion, dynamic road network adaptation, and reliance on black box mod els, this paper proposes SceneGTMM, a transferable GNN Transformer dual graph interaction map matching framework based on a conformal mapping based scene relative strategy. 1) Conformal mapping based scene relative strategy: constructs trajectory centric local coordinate systems to reduce dependence on the training road network, supporting cross regional transfer and dynamic road network updates; 2) GNN Transformer dual graph interaction architecture: a GNN modeled road graph captures local topological constraints, while a Transformer modeled trajectory graph captures global temporal dependencies, and cross graph attention achieves noise suppression and semantic alignment; 3) CRF enhanced structured prediction: combines the global context of the Transformer with the topological transition constraints of CRF to improve path connectivity and robustness. Experiments show that SceneGTM achieves over 80% accuracy on multi source trajectories with positioning errors of 16 50 meters, representing a 5.3% improvement over HMM. In cross city transfer scenarios, it outperforms MTrajRec, GraphMM, and TMM, and enhances interpretability through attention and relative coordinate visualization. This study provides a new paradigm for high precision, transferable map matching for real time traffic perception and autonomous driving path planning.

</details>


### [56] [Does Marginal Coverage Guarantee Class-Conditional Safety for Zero-Shot VLMs Under Shift?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19376)
*Jai Kumar Sharma,Amartya Dutta*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文审计了分裂共形预测（split-conformal prediction）作为零样本视觉-语言模型（VLM）拒绝层在分布偏移下的行为。发现边际覆盖率虽高（约0.86），但类别条件尾部覆盖率在ImageNet-Sketch上可崩溃至约0，且10-12%的类别低于有限样本零假设下限。源域诊断无法预测失效，源侧校准方法无法迁移，目标侧类别校准虽能提升尾部但需每类标签且代价高昂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分裂共形预测基于可交换性假设提供边际覆盖保证，正被广泛用作CLIP等零样本VLM的不确定性拒绝层。然而在部署（分布偏移）条件下，边际覆盖率可能掩盖类别尾部（最差类别）的严重失效，构成安全隐患，亟需系统性审计。

Method: 在CLIP、OpenCLIP、SigLIP上，跨ImageNet与非ImageNet设定，评估标准分裂共形、源侧Mondrian（类别条件）校准、clustered conformal及Conf-OT等方法在分布偏移下的边际覆盖率、类别条件尾部覆盖率、效率（set-size）等指标，并检验源域诊断指标的可迁移性。

Result: 边际覆盖率保持较高（约0.86）时，ImageNet-Sketch上最差类别覆盖率降至约0，10-12%类别低于有限样本零假设下限。失效与目标域类别准确率对齐但源域诊断无法预测。源侧Mondrian校准仅改善分布内尾部且不可迁移；clustered conformal和Conf-OT仅改善边际或平均指标。目标侧类别校准提升尾部但需每类标签且set-size代价高。SigLIP的sigmoid分数破坏APS的概率质量解释，存在2-3倍跨家族效率差距。结论在模型规模、预训练语料、提示、α及非ImageNet偏移设定下均稳健。

Conclusion: 边际共形覆盖率应被视为平均可靠性统计量，而非类别尾部的安全保证。在部署偏移场景下，依靠边际覆盖率作为VLM拒绝层的安全依据是不可靠的，需要开发针对类别尾部的校准与认证方法。

Abstract: Split-conformal prediction provides marginal coverage under exchangeability and is increasingly used as an abstention layer for zero-shot vision-language models (VLMs). We audit this practice under deployment shift for CLIP, OpenCLIP, and SigLIP across ImageNet and non-ImageNet settings. Marginal coverage can remain relatively high while class-conditional tail coverage collapses: on ImageNet-Sketch, worst-class coverage falls to $\approx 0$ and 10-12% of classes lie below a finite-sample null floor, despite marginal coverage of about 0.86. The failure is aligned with target-domain class accuracy but is not predicted by the source-domain diagnostics we test. Source-side Mondrian calibration improves the in-distribution tail but does not transfer, while clustered conformal and Conf-OT improve marginal or average metrics without recovering the worst-class tail. Target-side class calibration substantially lifts the tail, but requires labels for every class and remains set-size-intensive. We further identify a 2-3$\times$ cross-family efficiency gap and show that native SigLIP sigmoid scores remove APS's probability-mass interpretation. The findings persist across the tested model scale, pretraining corpus, prompt, miscoverage level $α$, and shifted non-ImageNet settings. Marginal conformal coverage should therefore be treated as an average reliability statistic, not as a safety guarantee for the class tail.

</details>


### [57] [CAViAR: A Causal Video Dataset for Fine-Grained Accident Reasoning in Real-World Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19380)
*Sparsh Garg,Yi-Wen Chen,Vijay Kumar B G,Abhishek Aich*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CAViAR（因果事故视频与事件分析库），一个包含2,249个真实行车记录仪事故视频的人工标注基准，用于评估视觉语言模型在高层次因果推理（如事故责任判定和交通规则违反类别识别）上的能力。基准测试发现当前VLM存在"感知-推理差距"：在光照等低层感知任务上表现良好，但在事故类型和责任推理上显著退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代自动驾驶系统在感知任务（目标检测、轨迹预测）上表现出色，但缺乏解释交通事故所需的高层次因果推理能力，尤其是责任判定（谁有过错、违反了哪条交通规则）在当前基准中基本未被探索。现有benchmark无法评估模型对事故因果关系的理解。

Method: 构建了CAViAR基准，包含从CarCrashDataset和Nexar收集的2,249个真实事故视频，每个视频带有结构化标注，涵盖环境条件、事故类型、因果解释、明显过错方、受影响方和明显违反规则类别。使用多数类/随机基线结合平衡指标来抵消类别不平衡影响，并在该基准上评估了Cosmos-Reason2、Qwen3-VL和InternVL3等SOTA视觉语言模型。

Result: 模型在感知能力上表现不均衡：光照条件几乎已解决，而天气和道路状况的准确性等于或低于多数类基线；所有模型在事故类型和责任推理上均显著下降，揭示了它们无法可靠地将可见的代理行为映射到规则相关的责任类别。

Conclusion: CAViAR揭示了当前VLM存在实用性的"感知-推理差距"：模型能识别显著上下文信息，但无法在安全关键的驾驶场景中可靠地将可见行为映射到标注的规则相关责任类别。该基准填补了事故因果推理评估的空白，代码和标注等资源已开源。

Abstract: While modern autonomous driving systems excel at perception tasks such as object detection and trajectory prediction, they lack the high-level causal reasoning required to interpret traffic accidents. In particular, determining responsibility, such as identifying who is at fault and which traffic rule was violated, remains largely unexplored in current benchmarks. To this end, we introduce CAViAR (Causal Accident Video and Incident Analysis Repository), a human-annotated dashcam benchmark comprising 2,249 real-world accident videos collected from CarCrashDataset (CCD) and Nexar. Each video is annotated with structured labels spanning environmental conditions, accident type, causal explanation, apparent At-Fault Agent, affected agent, and apparent rule-violation category. We benchmark state-of-the-art vision-language models (VLMs), including Cosmos-Reason2, Qwen3-VL, and InternVL3. Once class imbalance is accounted for with majority/random baselines and balanced metrics, perceptual competence is uneven--lighting is nearly solved, whereas weather and road-condition accuracy fall at or below the majority-class baseline---and all models degrade sharply on accident type and responsibility reasoning. Overall, CAViAR exposes a practical Perception--Reasoning Gap: current VLMs may recognize salient context, but do not reliably map visible agent actions to annotated rule-relevant responsibility categories in safety-critical driving scenarios. Code, annotation schema, prompts, and evaluation scripts are available at: https://github.com/nec-labs-ma/CAViAR

</details>


### [58] [Beyond Recognition: Compact Multi-Domain Arabic Manuscript HTR with Candidate-Selection Analysis and Evidence-Preserving Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19385)
*Abdullah Ahmed Ali,Mohammed Thamer Abdulhadi,Ali Haider Safaa,Dhulfiqar Mahdi Wadi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Phoenix（一个499万参数的CNN-BiLSTM-CTC手写文本识别器）和Athar（一个基于证据感知的审阅工作流）。Phoenix通过文档感知重放、扩展的81符号编解码器和遗忘防护机制在多个历史手稿域上进行迁移。在独立对照实验中，Phoenix在两个大规模测试集上将CER从22.12%/17.72%降至17.86%/11.84%，字符加权CER下降25.3%；Athar通过保留视觉阅读、展示受限替代方案、保守使用语言模型与导出可审计的TEI/PAGE-XML记录，将手稿HTR视为可审计的证据管理而非静默的文本替换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史阿拉伯语手稿转录不仅是一个识别问题，一个可用的学术系统必须应对多变的笔迹和版式、保留不确定的阅读结果、区分视觉证据与语言合理性，并记录研究者的最终决策。现有系统往往只关注识别准确率，忽视了学术工作流中对证据保留和审计性的需求。

Method: 采用CNN-BiLSTM-CTC架构构建499万参数的Phoenix识别器，通过文档感知重放（document-aware replay）在档案、马格里布和历史手稿域间进行适应；扩展至81符号编解码器；设计遗忘防护机制（forgetting guards）拒绝那些在提升新领域性能时对旧领域造成不可接受损失的检查点。Athar工作流保留视觉读取、暴露受限替代方案、保守使用局部语言模型、检索源文本平行语料并带有唯一/模糊/弃权状态，最终导出可审计的TEI和PAGE-XML记录。

Result: 在预先指定的保留集对照实验中（冻结评测前检查点、贪心解码与原始参考评分），Phoenix将Agapet数据集的CER从22.12%降至17.86%（10,594行），Omar数据集从17.72%降至11.84%（11,684行），TariMa数据集从10.39%微升到10.72%（164行）。两个大规模保留集的字符加权CER从19.98%降至14.93%，相对错误减少25.3%。开发诊断中Phoenix在四个可比域中均获得最低CER（9.59%未加权宏CER）。N-best诊断显示束搜索与Oracle@25之间存在2.15个百分点的oracle差距，而神经文本重排器、共识MBR、CTC后验质量估计和局部pre-CTC隐状态质量估计仅恢复了不到4%的差距。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持将手稿HTR评价为可审计的证据管理而非静默的文本替换。Phoenix在跨域适应中取得了显著的识别精度提升，但N-best解码的oracle差距表明编解码器仍存在信息瓶颈；Athar工作流通过证据感知设计（保留视觉读取、展示替代方案、记录决策）回应了学术手稿转录中超越纯识别的核心需求。

Abstract: Historical Arabic manuscript transcription is not only a recognition problem. A usable scholarly system must cope with shifting hands and layouts, preserve uncertain readings, distinguish visual evidence from linguistic plausibility, and record the researcher's final decision. We present Phoenix, a 4.99-million-parameter CNN-BiLSTM-CTC recognizer, and Athar, an evidence-aware review workflow built around it. Phoenix is adapted across archival, Maghrebi, and historical manuscript domains using document-aware replay, an expanded 81-symbol codec, and forgetting guards that reject checkpoints that improve a new domain at unacceptable cost to previous domains. In a pre-specified held-out comparison against the preceding checkpoint, frozen before evaluation and scored with greedy decoding and raw references, Phoenix reduced CER from 22.12% to 17.86% on 10,594 Agapet lines and from 17.72% to 11.84% on 11,684 Omar lines, while regressing from 10.39% to 10.72% on 164 TariMa lines. Across the two large held-out sets, character-weighted CER fell from 19.98% to 14.93%, a 25.3% relative error reduction. A separate same-protocol development diagnostic found the lowest CER for Phoenix on four of four comparable domains (9.59% unweighted macro CER). An N-best diagnostic revealed a 2.15-point oracle gap between beam decoding and Oracle@25, while neural text rerankers, consensus MBR, CTC-posterior quality estimation, and local pre-CTC hidden-state quality estimation recovered less than 4% of this gap. Athar therefore preserves the visual reading, exposes bounded alternatives, uses local language models conservatively, retrieves source parallels with unique, ambiguous, or abstain states, and exports auditable TEI and PAGE-XML records. The results support evaluating manuscript HTR as auditable evidence management rather than silent text replacement.

</details>


### [59] [HiRA-CAM: Preserving Fine-Grained Spatial Relevance in Gradient-Based Visual Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19407)
*Manasi Nerurkar,Ali A. Minai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出HiRA-CAM，一种改进的基于梯度的CNN可解释性方法，通过自适应利用CNN所有层的激活图生成更聚焦的显著性图，在目标分类任务上优于LayerCAM和Grad-CAM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型参数量巨大，难以解释其内部变换和输出；由于AI在关键应用中的使用，可解释性日益重要。现有方法如LayerCAM和Grad-CAM在生成CNN显著性图方面仍有改进空间。

Method: 在LayerCAM基础上提出HiRA-CAM，核心特点是自适应地利用CNN所有层的激活图，以生成更聚焦的显著性图。

Result: HiRA-CAM在生成目标分类的有用显著性图方面优于LayerCAM和Grad-CAM。

Conclusion: HiRA-CAM通过多层级激活图的自适应融合，显著提升了CNN可解释性，为目标分类提供了更有效的显著性图。

Abstract: Deep Learning models can include billions of parameters or more, making it difficult to explain their internal transformations and outputs. However, explainability is increasing in importance due to the use of AI in crucial applications. This paper focuses on the interpretability of convolutional neural networks (CNNs). Building on the popular gradient based method LayerCAM for extracting internal features in CNNs, we propose an improved method named HiRA-CAM, and show that it outperforms both LayerCAM and Grad-CAM on creating useful saliency maps for object classification. The main feature of HiRA-CAM is its adaptive use of activation maps from all the layers of the CNN to arrive at a more focused saliency map.

</details>

*Luis F. Gomez,Julian Fierrez,Roberto Daza,Ruben Tolosana,Aythami Morales,Gonzalo Garrido,Javier Rueda,Enrique Navarro*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍VideoRun2D Demo，一个利用不同人体姿态估计器对短跑进行生物力学分析的系统，使用44名专业跑者的314次短跑数据，重点分析髋关节和膝关节的屈伸角度。最佳跟踪器的平均均方根误差为5.83°至11.46°，结合后处理模块后可降至5.30°和9.87°，表明人体姿态追踪方法可用于跑步生物力学分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人体姿态估计技术因深度学习模型、数据量和计算资源的发展而显著进步，能够实现高精度的身体追踪系统，在运动分析和绩效评估中有直接应用价值。本文旨在探索将这些姿态估计技术应用于短跑运动中的生物力学分析。

Method: VideoRun2D Demo框架使用不同的人体姿态估计器对短跑动作进行生物力学分析，以专家手动标注为基准进行评估。框架利用44名专业跑者的314次短跑数据，聚焦两个关键关节角度（髋关节屈伸和膝关节屈伸），并包含一个用于异常值检测的后处理模块。

Result: 测试结果表明，最佳跟踪器的平均均方根误差范围为11.46°到5.83°；结合后处理模块后，误差可分别降至9.87°和5.30°。

Conclusion: VideoRun2D Demo的研究发现表明，人体姿态追踪方法可以成为跑步生物力学分析的宝贵工具资源。

Abstract: Human pose estimation has advanced significantly due to the development of deep learning models, increased data availability, and improved computing resources. These developments have led to highly accurate body tracking systems with direct applications in sports analysis and performance evaluation. The VideoRun2D Demo performs a biomechanical analysis during sprints using different human pose estimators. The proposed framework was evaluated using human pose trackers and expert manual annotations. The tested framework uses 314 sprints from 44 professional runners, focusing on two key joint angles in sprint biomechanics: 1) hip flexion/extension and 2) knee flexion/extension. The framework also includes a post-processing module for outlier detection. The tested results demonstrate that the average root-mean-square errors range from 11.46° to 5.83° for the best trackers. When integrated with the post-processing modules, these errors can be reduced to 9.87° and 5.30°, respectively. The VideoRun2D Demo findings suggest that human pose-tracking approaches can be valuable resources for the biomechanical analysis of running.

</details>


### [61] [A Plug-in Interpretation of Conditioning in Score-Based Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19504)
*Libo Chen,Souvik Ghosh,Teo Deveney,Chris Budd,Vinay P. Namboodiri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多速度联合扩散的条件生成机制，通过无条件联合分数网络与插件校正项实现条件生成，并引入log-Fokker-Planck残差正则化改善ODE求解质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型的条件生成方法通常依赖条件分数网络或在采样时进行引导，缺乏对条件如何影响生成过程的透明解释。作者希望设计一种机制，能够将条件贡献从无条件动力学中显式分离，从而提供清晰的理解视角，同时保证条件采样的原则性与可比性。

Method: 该方法基于目标与条件（condition）的多速度联合扩散，学习一个无条件的联合分数网络。在推理阶段，通过插件式（plug-in）校正项强制执行条件约束，该校正项将条件贡献从无条件动力学中显式分离出来。在此基础上推导出显式的条件反向时间SDE和近似的概率流ODE。为减小ODE与SDE之间的差距，引入log-Fokker-Planck残差正则化以提升确定性ODE采样质量。

Result: 在条件图像生成任务上取得了有竞争力的性能，验证了插件式条件生成视角的有效性。额外的ODE-SDE对比实验表明，log-Fokker-Planck残差正则化能够显著改善确定性ODE采样的质量。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了条件扩散模型的一种新范式，通过插件校正项透明地展示了条件对生成过程的引导机制，并利用正则化手段弥合了ODE与SDE采样之间的差距，为条件生成任务的确定性采样提供了改进方案。

Abstract: We propose a conditioning mechanism for diffusion models based on multi-speed joint diffusion of the target and the condition. The mechanism learns an unconditional joint score network and enforces conditioning at inference via a plug-in correction term. The plug-in term separates the conditioning contribution from the learned unconditional dynamics, offering a transparent view of how the condition steers generation of the target distribution. Building on this, we derive explicit conditional reverse-time SDEs and approximate probability-flow ODEs, enabling principled and directly comparable conditional samplers. To reduce the induced ODE--SDE discrepancy, we introduce a log-Fokker--Planck residual regularization that improves ODE sampling quality. Experiments on conditional image generation tasks demonstrate competitive performance and support the effectiveness of the plug-in conditioning view. Additional ODE--SDE comparison experiments show that the log-Fokker--Planck residual regularization improves deterministic ODE sampling.

</details>


### [62] [CVSD-Reg: Cross-Modal Visual Semantic Prior Distillation for Robust LiDAR Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19536)
*Eunsoo Im,Junghun Suh,Gyeonggwan Lee,Seunghwan Hong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CVSD-Reg是一种鲁棒的全局LiDAR点云配准框架，通过从视觉基础模型(DINOv2)中蒸馏视觉语义先验到LiDAR表示中，解决了现有几何方法对点密度、扫描模式、视角和传感器特性敏感的问题。该方法在KITTI、nuScenes和HeLiPR数据集上分别达到97.7%、99.0%和99.3%的严格成功率，在稀疏16-beam Velodyne扫描上达到97.3%，性能优于最先进的几何配准方法最多44.0个百分点，且推理时完全无需相机输入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的全局点云配准方法依赖几何表示，对点密度、扫描模式、视角和传感器特性的变化高度敏感，这限制了其在真实跨传感器场景中的泛化能力。作者希望利用视觉基础模型中的语义先验来增强LiDAR表示的鲁棒性，从而克服纯几何方法的局限性。

Method: CVSD-Reg采用两阶段训练框架。第一阶段：Point Transformer V3学生模型通过对比蒸馏和球面流形对齐从冻结的DINOv2教师模型中学习视觉语义先验，保持教师嵌入空间的超球面几何结构；通过自监督InfoNCE一致性和软SE(3)不变性增强视角鲁棒描述子。第二阶段：将蒸馏得到的表示通过对应学习、密度感知点丢弃增强和端到端位姿优化适配到配准任务。推理时完全不需要相机输入。

Result: 在KITTI、nuScenes和HeLiPR数据集上，CVSD-Reg的严格成功率(SR@0.5m/1°)分别达到97.7%、99.0%和99.3%；在稀疏16-beam Velodyne扫描上达到97.3%。相比最先进的几何配准方法，性能提升最多达44.0个百分点，且无需相机输入或ICP后处理优化。单个检查点即可同时适用于单传感器和零样本跨传感器场景。

Conclusion: 通过从视觉基础模型蒸馏语义先验到LiDAR表示，CVSD-Reg显著提升了全局LiDAR配准在跨传感器和稀疏扫描场景下的鲁棒性与泛化能力，展示了跨模态知识蒸馏在3D几何任务中的巨大潜力，为无需相机辅助的鲁棒LiDAR配准提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Learning-based global point cloud registration has achieved remarkable progress, yet its reliance on geometric representations makes existing methods sensitive to variations in point density, scan pattern, viewpoint, and sensor characteristics. We propose CVSD-Reg, a robust global LiDAR registration framework that distills visual semantic priors from a vision foundation model into LiDAR representations. In Stage 1, a Point Transformer V3 student learns from a frozen DINOv2 teacher through contrastive distillation and spherical-manifold alignment, which preserves the hyperspherical geometry of the teacher embedding space. Self-supervised InfoNCE consistency and soft $\mathrm{SE}(3)$ invariance further encourage viewpoint-robust descriptors. In Stage 2, the distilled representation is adapted to registration through correspondence learning, density-aware point-dropout augmentation, and end-to-end pose optimization. With a single checkpoint, CVSD-Reg generalizes to both single-sensor and zero-shot cross-sensor scenarios without sensor-specific adaptation and remains entirely camera-free at inference. On KITTI, nuScenes, and HeLiPR, CVSD-Reg achieves strict success rate (SR@0.5\,m/$1^\circ$) of 97.7$\%$, 99.0$\%$, and 99.3$\%$, respectively, including 97.3$\%$ on sparse 16-beam Velodyne scans. It outperforms state-of-the-art geometric registration methods by up to 44.0 percentage points without requiring camera inputs or post-hoc ICP refinement.

</details>


### [63] [Where Grounding Accuracy Lives on the IoU Curve: Label-Free Inference-Time Boundary Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19553)
*Bo Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种名为LFPR（Label-free Precision Refinement）的无标注精确定位优化方法：冻结的直接问答模型利用自身预测来分配一次额外的局部观察，通过将预测的小区域路由到更高分辨率、在上下文裁剪中重新接地表达式、在固定几何守卫下接纳候选，并返回坐标级中点，从而提升指代表达的边界框精度，且无需访问目标标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型常能识别正确所指对象，但返回的边界框不够精确。作者希望在不借助目标标注、不微调模型的前提下，利用模型自身的预测能力进行一次额外的局部观察来细化边界框，从而部分解耦"所指选择"与"边界精度"两个任务。

Method: LFPR包含四个组成部分：(1) 路由：将预测为小的区域送入高分辨率通道；(2) 在上下文裁剪内重新接地表达式；(3) 仅在固定几何守卫（geometric guards）下接纳候选框；(4) 返回固定的坐标逐维中点。整个流程基于冻结的模型，无需目标标注。

Result: 在三类证据层面报告结果：回顾性Ref-L4（31,921条）上mAcc_{0.5:0.95}从72.947%提升至76.013%；冻结迁移到RefCOCO/RefCOCO+/RefCOCOg在Acc@0.5、mAcc和mean IoU上全部提升（mAcc +0.645，Acc@0.5 +0.817），但Acc@0.9总体不变（路由+1.162，但裁剪/守卫/融合回吐-1.192）；前瞻性图像不重叠的Flickr30K Entities评估所有端点提升（mAcc +0.973，Acc@0.9 +1.022），单框变体更强（mAcc +2.575，Acc@0.9 +3.689）；应用于两个已发布的定位专家模型也全端点提升（Acc@0.9 +1.569/+6.716），代价约两倍延迟；无守卫对照在所有指标上弱于原方案，表明几何守卫是承重的关键组件。

Conclusion: 所指选择与边界精度是部分可分离的两个能力，不同组件会移动IoU曲线相反的区域——这是单一阈值无法揭示的行为。LFPR可作为与专家定位模型训练互补的即插即用策略。

Abstract: Vision--language models can identify the correct referent while returning an imprecise bounding box. We study whether a frozen direct-answer model can use its own prediction to allocate one additional localized observation without accessing target annotations at inference. Label-free precision refinement (LFPR) routes predicted-small regions to a higher-resolution pass, re-grounds the expression inside a context crop, admits a candidate only under fixed geometric guards, and returns a fixed coordinate-wise midpoint. We report results across three evidence tiers. On 31,921 retrospective Ref-L4 expressions, LFPR raises mAcc$_{0.5:0.95}$ from 72.947\% to 76.013\% (Acc@0.5 88.531\%$\to$89.725\%, Acc@0.9 55.788\%$\to$61.142\%). A frozen transfer to 30,969 RefCOCO/RefCOCO+/RefCOCOg expressions improves every dataset at Acc@0.5, mAcc, and mean IoU (pooled mAcc $+0.645$, Acc@0.5 $+0.817$), while Acc@0.9 is unchanged overall: routing alone gains $+1.162$ points there, but crop, guards, and fusion give back $-1.192$, offsetting rather than showing no strict-IoU effect. A prospective, image-disjoint Flickr30K Entities evaluation improves every endpoint (mAcc $+0.973$, Acc@0.9 $+1.022$), more strongly under a single-box variant (mAcc $+2.575$, Acc@0.9 $+3.689$). The same operator applied to two released grounding specialists improves every endpoint (Acc@0.9 $+1.569$/$+6.716$ for EGM-4B/8B) at roughly twice the latency, composing with specialist training rather than replacing it. A genuine unguarded control (guard removed from the same candidates) underperforms the incumbent on every metric, showing the guard is load-bearing. Together, these results show that referent selection and boundary precision are partially separable, with different components moving opposing regions of the IoU curve -- behavior a single threshold cannot reveal.

</details>


### [64] [Stream4D: 4D-Consistency for Streaming Autoregressive Diffusion Video Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19556)
*Yuanhao Ban,Jiaqi Feng,Hengguang Zhou,Xiaohuan Pei,Justin Cui,Cho-Jui Hsieh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Stream4D 提出一种用于流式自回归扩散模型的4D重建奖励方法，通过显式建模场景动态来替代静态3D重建批评器，解决长时序视频生成中的几何漂移和运动退化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式自回归扩散模型虽然支持实时、长时序视频生成，但其训练目标仅优化局部帧预测，忽视了世界的几何与动态一致性，导致长序列中几何漂移累积，视频退化为静态或非自然运动。已有的双向方法使用基于3D高斯泼溅重建的奖励信号，但静态3D重建无法建模动态场景，会错误惩罚真实物体运动，并因冻结视频而获得最高奖励，这一捷径在自回归设置中尤为有害。

Method: Stream4D 包含三个组成部分：(1) 用前馈4D重建奖励替代静态批评器，显式建模场景动态，使连贯运动获得高一致性奖励；(2) 引入运动先验，奖励自然的场景流幅度，同时惩罚抖动和非刚体伪影；(3) 结合轻量级感知锚点，构成完整的训练配方。

Result: 在多种自回归视频骨干网络和不同生成时长下，Stream4D 提升了4D重建质量，更有效地保持运动，并获得了更高的人类对齐偏好评分。

Conclusion: Stream4D 通过动态4D重建奖励加运动先验的组合，有效解决了流式自回归视频生成中的几何漂移与运动退化问题，为长时序动态视频生成提供了更优的训练范式。

Abstract: Streaming autoregressive diffusion models enable real-time, long-horizon video generation, but their training objectives optimize local frame prediction rather than the geometry and dynamics of a coherent world: long rollouts accumulate geometric drift and degrade into static or unnatural motion. Recent bidirectional approaches address this problem using rewards signals built upon 3D Gaussian-Splatting reconstruction. However, a single rigid 3d reconstruction cannot model a dynamic scene, so this critic penalizes genuine object motion as reconstruction error and is maximized by freezing the video. This shortcut is especially detrimental in the AR setting, where each chunk can propagate an already-static configuration. In this work, we propose Stream4D, which replaces the static critic with a feed-forward 4D reconstruction reward that explicitly models scene dynamics, allowing coherent motion to receive high consistency rewards. To further guide motion magnitude and quality, we add a motion prior that rewards natural scene-flow magnitude while penalizing jitter and non-rigid artifacts. Our final recipe combines these two terms with a lightweight perceptual anchor. Across various autoregressive video backbones and various generation horizons, Stream4D improves 4D reconstruction quality, preserves motion more effectively, and achieves higher human-aligned preference. Project page: https://banyuanhao.github.io/Stream4D/

</details>


### [65] [Block3D: Efficient Text-to-3D Generation via Block-Wise Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19567)
*Bowen Cui,Weijie Wang,Zeyu Zhang,Yefei He,Mingda Lin,Haoyu Zhao,Yuanyu He,Donny Y. Chen,Feng Chen,Bohan Zhuang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Block3D，一种块级扩散框架，将离散形状token序列划分为连续块，以自回归方式逐块生成，并在当前块内联合去噪所有token。通过引入基于置信度的块内校正机制来缓解误差累积。在TRELLIS-500K的留出集上，Block3D将端到端生成时间从25.71秒降至4.99秒，实现5.15倍加速，且不牺牲几何保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到3D生成方法存在固有局限：自回归解码是顺序的且无法修正错误；扩散和流匹配模型反复处理完整表示，导致高质量生成的推理成本高昂。如何在保证几何保真度的同时大幅降低推理成本是核心挑战。

Method: Block3D采用块级扩散框架，将离散形状token序列分割为连续块，以自回归方式逐块生成，同时用扩散方式联合去噪当前块内的所有token。为缓解误差累积，引入基于置信度的块内校正机制，在每块被最终确定之前对低置信度token进行修正。

Result: 在TRELLIS-500K的留出集上，Block3D将平均端到端生成时间从25.71秒降至4.99秒，实现5.15倍加速，同时不损失几何保真度。

Conclusion: Block3D通过块级自回归扩散框架有效平衡了生成效率与几何质量，置信度引导的块内校正机制成功缓解了误差累积问题，为高质量、低成本的文本到3D生成提供了新方案。
