TL;DR: 本文提出了AdvSpot，这是首个用于对抗性OCR评估的基准，包含390张图像和区域级标注，覆盖5个主要类别和13种细粒度对抗OCR类型。同时提出ArmorOCR两阶段训练框架，通过On-Policy Self-Distillation（OPSD）获取对抗OCR感知能力，并结合GRPO优化精炼grounded OCR感知，在保持通用OCR能力的同时显著提升对抗性OCR感知性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型多模态模型虽展现出强大的OCR识别能力，但对人可读而模型难以定位和识别的对抗性视觉文本仍存在脆弱性。现有OCR基准主要关注自然场景或文档风格文本，而对抗性OCR评估在规模、任务覆盖或区域感知评估方面十分有限，缺乏系统性的基准来评估和推动模型在对抗性OCR场景下的表现。

Method: 提出两阶段训练框架ArmorOCR：(1) 第一阶段通过On-Policy Self-Distillation（OPSD）从特权变换观测中蒸馏获取缺失的对抗OCR感知能力；(2) 第二阶段通过Group Relative Policy Optimization（GRPO）结合任务条件奖励，对定位、识别、完整定位（full spotting）和视觉问答（VQA）等多方面grounded OCR感知进行优化。

Result: 在AdvSpot基准以及其他对抗性OCR基准和通用OCR基准上的实验表明，ArmorOCR在保持竞争性通用OCR能力的同时，持续有效地提升了对抗性OCR感知性能。

Conclusion: 该研究将对抗性OCR形式化为grounded OCR感知任务，为对抗性OCR评估提供了首个标准化基准AdvSpot，并验证了ArmorOCR训练框架在增强LMM对抗性OCR感知鲁棒性方面的有效性，为后续对抗性OCR研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Large multimodal models (LMMs) have demonstrated strong OCR recognition capabilities, yet remain vulnerable to adversarial visual text that is readable to humans but challenging for models to localize and recognize. Existing OCR benchmarks mainly focus on natural or document-style text, while adversarial OCR evaluations remain limited in scale, task coverage, or region-aware evaluation. In this paper, we formulate adversarial OCR as a \textbf{grounded OCR perception} task and introduce \textbf{AdvSpot}, the first benchmark for grounded adversarial OCR evaluation. AdvSpot comprises 390 images with region-level annotations, spanning 5 primary categories and 13 fine-grained adversarial OCR types. To address this challenge, we propose \textbf{ArmorOCR}, a two-stage training framework for robust adversarial OCR perception. ArmorOCR first acquires missing adversarial OCR perception from privileged transformed observations through On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD), and then refines grounded OCR perception through Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with task-conditioned rewards for localization, recognition, full spotting, and visual question answering (VQA). Experiments on our AdvSpot, other adversarial OCR benchmarks, and general OCR benchmarks demonstrate that ArmorOCR consistently improves adversarial OCR perception while preserving competitive general OCR capability.

</details>


### [99] [ID-VTG: Image-Disambiguated Video Temporal Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20127)
*Minghang Zheng,Jingli Wei,Hongyi Yang,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出图像消歧视频时间定位（ID-VTG）任务，利用参考图像+文本的多模态查询精确定位视频中特定实例执行描述动作的片段。构建了两个基准数据集（IDVTG-Gym和IDVTG-InternVid），并提出基于双分支快慢架构的VGD-Agg框架，通过Compare Token和Depress Value增强样本可区分性，在基准上取得SOTA结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的视频时间定位（VTG）在自然语言查询需要区分多个涉及视觉相似实体的事件时面临挑战，尤其当依赖难以用文字准确描述的细粒度视觉属性时更为困难。现有方法仅使用文本查询，无法充分区分外观相似的实体。

Method: 提出ID-VTG任务，将参考图像与文本描述结合作为多模态查询。构建两个基准：IDVTG-Gym（细粒度体操动作、相似服装运动员）和IDVTG-InternVid（开放世界、多样实体、时间干扰）。提出VGD-Agg框架，采用双分支快慢架构：快分支高效生成初步事件候选，慢分支进行视频帧与参考图像间的细粒度帧级匹配。通过两个可学习token增强判别能力：Compare Token代表硬负样本以探测目标实例是否存在，Depress Value代表与文本无关的事件，将缺乏目标实例的候选推向Depress Value以简化文本消歧。

Result: 在两个提出的基准数据集上进行了广泛实验，VGD-Agg框架取得了最先进的性能表现。

Conclusion: ID-VTG为VTG领域引入了图像辅助消歧的新范式，VGD-Agg框架通过显式建模硬负样本和无关事件，有效解决了视觉相似实体间的区分难题，为多模态视频定位提供了新的研究方向。

Abstract: Video Temporal Grounding (VTG) faces significant challenges when natural language queries must distinguish between multiple events involving visually similar entities, particularly when relying on fine-grained visual attributes that are difficult to describe accurately in words alone. To address this, we introduce Image-Disambiguated Video Temporal Grounding (ID-VTG), a task that leverages multimodal queries combining a reference image and a text description to precisely localize segments where a specific instance performs a described action. To facilitate research, we construct two benchmarks: IDVTG-Gym, focusing on fine-grained, compositionally ordered gymnastics actions with athletes in similar uniforms; and IDVTG-InternVid, an open-world dataset featuring diverse entities (e.g., humans, animals, fictional characters) and significant temporal distractors. Methodologically, we propose the Visually-Guided Disambiguation Aggregation (VGD-Agg) framework based on a dual-branch fast-slow architecture. The fast branch efficiently generates preliminary event proposals, while the slow branch performs fine-grained frame-level matching between video frames and the reference image. We enhance discriminability via two learnable tokens: a Compare Token, which represents hard negatives to probe for the presence of the target instance (as referred to by the query image), and a Depress Value, which represents text-irrelevant events. Proposals that the Compare Token identifies as lacking the target instance are pushed toward the Depress Value, thus easing disambiguation via the text query. Extensive experiments validate our approach, which achieves state-of-the-art results on the proposed benchmarks. Code is available at https://github.com/oceanflowlab/ID-VTG.

</details>


### [100] [Feature Evolution and Migration during Vision Transformer Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20134)
*Joonas Järve,Halil Ibrahim Aysel,Tarun Khajuria,Meelis Kull*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的视角来研究Vision Transformer (ViT)中的特征演化，通过在网络深度（层）和训练时间（epoch）两个维度上可视化训练过程。使用稀疏自编码器（SAEs）从CLS-token表示中提取候选稀疏特征，并比较不同epoch-layer对之间的激活分布，从而揭示特征级别的动态变化。实验发现特征迁移集中在训练早期，更多向浅层迁移而非深层，且随着特征组织稳定而减弱；深层比浅层更早、更强地稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的ViT特征研究主要依赖于表示层面的相似性度量，无法直接观察到特征级别的动态变化。作者希望通过特征级别的分析来深入理解ViT在训练过程中如何学习和演化特征，填补表示层面分析无法揭示的细节空白。

Method: 使用Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs)从CLS-token表示中提取稀疏特征，然后在epoch和layer组成的二维网格上比较特征的激活分布。通过特征激活的时序与空间变化来分析特征迁移现象，即在训练过程中特征最可检测的层发生变化的情况。

Result: 实验结果显示：特征迁移主要集中在训练早期；迁移更倾向于向浅层发生而非深层；随着训练进行特征组织趋于稳定，迁移现象减少；深层网络层比浅层更早且更强烈地实现稳定。

Conclusion: 该框架可以作为理解ViT学习与演化过程的有效工具，为特征级动态分析提供了新的方法路径，有望在模型可解释性和训练动态理解方面做出贡献。

Abstract: We present a novel view on feature evolution in Vision Transformers (ViTs) by visualizing the training process over two dimensions -- network depth (layer) and training time (epochs). We employ Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) to extract candidate sparse features from CLS-token representations and compare their activation profiles across epoch--layer pairs. This allows us to study feature-level dynamics that are not directly visible from representation-level similarity measures. Furthermore, we demonstrate how this framework of feature evolution allows us to describe feature migration, the change in the layer where a feature is most detectable during training. Our experiments show that migration is concentrated early in training, occurs more often toward earlier layers than toward deeper layers, and declines as feature organization stabilizes. We further find that deeper layers stabilize earlier and more strongly than shallow layers. The results show that our approach can be employed as a tool for understanding how ViTs learn and evolve.

</details>


### [101] [Prompt-Conditioned Channel Attention for Hierarchical Feature Modulation toward Anatomy-Agnostic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20229)
*Mosharof Hossain,Md Rabiul Islam,Limon Halder,Erchin Serpedin,Md Kamrul Hasan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Prompt-Conditioned Channel Attention（PCCA）机制及基于此构建的PROMISE-Net网络（含PROMISE-CNN和PROMISE-Txformer两种变体），通过在海量特征表示层级中实现提示条件化的通道级调制，解决医学图像分割中低对比度、模糊边界等问题。在四个基准数据集上的实验表明，相较于基线U-Net和UNETR，两种变体均取得一致的IoU提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交互式分割方法主要通过晚期融合的方式整合提示信息，缺乏在层级特征表示上进行提示驱动的通道级显式调制机制，难以捕捉更深层的上下文和模态特异的变异，限制了分割模型在结构模糊区域的定位能力。

Method: PCCA通过池化提取紧凑的通道描述符，将其投影到共享空间，并通过门控激发机制融合以计算提示感知的通道注意力权重。这些权重在多个网络阶段自适应地重新校准特征响应，实现提示条件化的语义增强层级表示。基于此构建PROMISE-Net，包含卷积变体（PROMISE-CNN）和Transformer变体（PROMISE-Txformer）。

Result: 在ISIC-Lesion、Kvasir-Polyp、CAMUS-Cardiac和Kvasir-Instrument四个基准上，PROMISE-CNN相对U-Net基线的相对IoU提升分别为10.4%、8.7%、0.8%和3.4%；PROMISE-Txformer相对UNETR基线的相对IoU提升分别为7.6%、23.0%、2.1%和1.1%。

Conclusion: PCCA与PROMISE-Net在不同架构、成像模态和解剖目标上均展现出一致的性能提升，可作为医学图像分割中提示感知层级特征调制的可扩展、可泛化框架。

Abstract: Anatomically plausible segmentation remains challenging because of low contrast, ambiguous boundaries, and modality-specific artifacts. Interactive segmentation has emerged as a promising strategy to guide feature extraction and improve localization, particularly in structurally ambiguous regions. However, existing methods integrate prompts through late-stage fusion and lack explicit mechanisms for prompt-driven channel-wise modulation across hierarchical feature representations, limiting their ability to capture deeper contextual and modality-specific variations. To address these limitations, we introduce Prompt-Conditioned Channel Attention (PCCA), a novel modulation mechanism that enables deep, hierarchical integration of semantic prompts within encoder-decoder networks. PCCA extracts compact channel descriptors via pooling, projects them into a shared space, and fuses them through a gated excitation mechanism to compute prompt-aware channel attention weights. These weights adaptively recalibrate feature responses across multiple network stages, enabling prompt-conditioned, semantically enriched hierarchical representations. Building on this, we propose PROMISE-Net, instantiated in two network variants: a convolutional model (PROMISE-CNN) and a transformer-based model (PROMISE-Txformer). Across the ISIC-Lesion, Kvasir-Polyp, CAMUS-Cardiac, and Kvasir-Instrument benchmarks, integrating PCCA into PROMISE-CNN yielded relative IoU gains of 10.4%, 8.7%, 0.8%, and 3.4%, respectively, over the baseline U-Net, while PROMISE-Txformer achieved corresponding gains of 7.6%, 23.0%, 2.1%, and 1.1%, respectively, over the baseline UNETR. These results show consistent improvements across architectures, imaging modalities, and anatomical targets, establishing PCCA and PROMISE-Net as a scalable, generalizable framework for prompt-aware hierarchical feature modulation in medical image segmentation.

</details>


### [102] [Ultra-High-Definition Restoration Transformers with Correlation Matching Transformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20263)
*Cong Wang,Liyan Wang,Jinshan Pan,Wei Wang,Wenqi Ren,Jun Liu,Xiaochun Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UHDformer++ 是一个通用的基于 Transformer 的超高清（UHD）图像复原框架，它在四个协调的学习空间（高分辨率 HR、低分辨率 LR、超分辨率 SR 和低-高融合重构 LHFR）中运行，并通过 FR-CMT 和 ACM 两个模块桥接高-低分辨率空间。该方法在 5 种 UHD 复原任务（低光增强、去雾、去模糊、去雨、去雪）中，模型参数较最新方法至少减少 86%，同时获得显著的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UHD 图像复原（如去噪、去模糊、去雨等）在超高清分辨率下计算开销巨大，现有 Transformer 方法在大分辨率下参数量和计算成本过高，且难以在保持高分辨率细节的同时高效处理特征。作者旨在设计一个通用且轻量的 Transformer 框架，能够在多个 UHD 复原任务中兼顾性能与效率。

Method: UHDformer++ 采用四个协调的学习空间：HR 空间用于多层级特征提取并与 LR 线索融合生成残差图像；LR 空间从 HR 中蒸馏互补表征以提升复原质量；SR 空间对 LR 输出进行超分辨率放大，为 LHFR 提供更丰富的特征；LHFR 空间负责最终图像复原。此外引入两个桥接模块：FR-CMT 模块从融合了最大池化和平均池化的 HR 特征中选取最具信息量的通道（前 C/r 个）替换 LR Transformer 中信息量较低的通道；ACM 模块自适应地重新校准多尺度 HR 特征，确保仅任务相关信息传播到 LR 空间。

Result: 在 5 个 UHD 复原任务（低光增强、去雾、去模糊、去雨、去雪）上进行大量实验，UHDformer++ 相比近期最先进方法将模型参数减少至少 86%，同时获得显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: UHDformer++ 通过协同利用四个学习空间并设计 FR-CMT 和 ACM 两个轻量模块，有效解决了超高分辨率下 Transformer 方法参数量大、计算成本高的问题，在多个 UHD 复原任务上取得了高效且高性能的结果，验证了该框架作为通用 UHD 复原方案的可行性。

Abstract: We propose UHDformer++, a general Transformer-based framework to solve numerous Ultra-High-Definition (UHD) image restoration tasks. UHDformer++ operates across $4$ coordinated learning spaces: 1) a high-resolution space (HR) for multi-level feature extraction, 2) a low-resolution space (LR) for learning compact, representative features, 3) a super-resolution space (SR) for upsampling low-resolution features from SR, and 4) a low-high fusion and reconstruction space (LHFR) for final image restoration. Specifically, HR extracts multi-scale high-resolution features and fuses them with low-resolution cues to produce residual images, while LR distills complementary representations from HR to improve restoration quality. To supply LHFR with richer features, SR super-resolves LR outputs before fusion. We further introduce two modules to bridge the high- and low-resolution spaces. The Feature-Refined Correlation Matching Transformation (FR-CMT) module selects the top $C/r~(C~\text{denotes the number of channels;~}r\geq1~\text{controls the squeezing level})$, from the fusion between max- and mean-pooled high-resolution features to replace less informative channels in the low-resolution Transformer. The Adaptive Channel Modulator (ACM) adaptively recalibrates multi-scale high-resolution features, ensuring that only task-relevant information propagates to LR. Extensive experiments demonstrate that UHDformer++ reduces model parameters by at least 86\% compared with recent state-of-the-art methods while achieving substantial performance gains across $5$ UHD restoration tasks, including low-light image enhancement, dehazing, deblurring, deraining, and desnowing. Code will be released at https://github.com/supersupercong/uhdformerplus.

</details>


### [103] [Towards Surgical World-Action Modeling: A Preliminary Joint Visual-Trajectory Forecasting for Surgical Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20284)
*Weiliang Huang,Huanrong Liu,Bob Zhang,Qi Dou,Zhen Chen,Yun Gu,Guy Rosman,Qingbiao Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个初步的联合视觉-轨迹世界动作模型，同时预测未来手术画面的视觉状态和器械运动轨迹，采用分块自回归滚动预测策略，在15步预测中显著优于直接一步预测（首段PSNR从18.86提升至23.11 dB，ADE从45.77降至22.22像素），验证了联合视觉-运动预测的初步可行性，但长时预测仍存在视觉退化与轨迹误差累积的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手术规划方法将未来场景生成与器械轨迹预测视为两个独立任务：仅场景模型无法在轨迹层面直接评估器械运动准确性；仅轨迹模型无法捕捉器械运动带来的视觉后果，两者都无法保证预测轨迹与未来场景演化之间的一致性。联合预测两者可以提供更完整的动作-场景动力学描述。

Method: 将历史视频帧和器械轨迹编码为潜在表示，经时空编码器处理后，通过分离的视觉状态预测头和轨迹预测头解码输出。基于该初步架构，采用分块(chunked)自回归滚动方式重复预测未来15步。

Result: 分块策略在所有评估时域上均优于直接一次性预测，首段PSNR从18.86提升至23.11 dB，ADE从45.77像素降至22.22像素，验证了联合视觉-运动预测的初始可行性。

Conclusion: 联合视觉-轨迹预测具有可行性与优势，但长预测时域上存在渐进视觉退化与轨迹误差累积问题，是未来手术世界动作建模的重要挑战。

Abstract: Reliable surgical planning requires models to anticipate not only how instruments will move, but also how the operative visual state will evolve together with such motion. Existing approaches typically treat future scene generation and instrument trajectory prediction as two separate tasks. Scene-only models cannot directly evaluate the accuracy of future instrument motion at the trajectory level, while trajectory-only models fail to capture the visual consequences of instrument movement, leaving the consistency between predicted trajectories and future scene evolution unaddressed. Jointly forecasting both provides a more complete account of surgical action-scene dynamics by enabling explicit trajectory-level evaluation while simultaneously modeling the corresponding visual evolution. To bridge this gap, we present a preliminary joint visual-trajectory world-action model that simultaneously forecasts future visual states and instrument trajectories from historical surgical observations. Specifically, we encode historical video frames and tool trajectories into latent representations, which are processed by a temporal-spatial encoder and subsequently decoded through separate visual-state and trajectory prediction heads. Based on this preliminary architecture, a chunked autoregressive rollout is repeatedly applied to predict fifteen future steps. The chunked strategy consistently outperforms direct one-shot prediction across all evaluated horizons, improving first-segment PSNR from 18.86 to 23.11 dB and reducing ADE from 45.77 to 22.22 pixels. These results demonstrate the initial feasibility of joint visual-motion forecasting. However, we observe progressive visual degradation and accumulated trajectory errors over longer prediction horizons, which remain important challenges for future surgical world-action modeling.

</details>


### [104] [CalcSeg: Confidence-aware 3D Latent Context Curriculum Learning For Myocardial Scar Segmentation From Single-Stack LGE-CMRs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20305)
*Nivetha Jayakumar,Hannah Kim,Amit R. Patel,Miaomiao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CalcSeg，一种基于置信度感知的潜在上下文课程学习框架，用于从单栈LGE-CMR图像中进行心肌疤痕分割。通过动态半监督课程学习策略和潜在切片级自注意力机制，有效应对低组织对比度、弥散性小疤痕区域以及有限的3D空间上下文等挑战，在多中心临床数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心肌疤痕分割是临床重要但长期具有挑战性的任务，尤其在低组织对比度、弥散性及小型疤痕区域情况下表现不佳。单栈LGE-CMR成像缺少完整的3D空间上下文，加之标注数据有限，进一步加剧了分割难度。

Method: CalcSeg包含两个核心组件：(1) 动态半监督课程学习策略，利用置信度感知评分函数（整合预测疤痕图误差、认知不确定性和疤痕负担估计）自动评估样本难度并无标签地渐进扩展训练；(2) 潜在切片级自注意力机制，从稀疏2D输入中捕获切片间依赖关系并推断3D空间表示，提高分割鲁棒性。

Result: 在多中心临床LGE-CMR数据集上与现有疤痕分割网络进行基准对比，CalcSeg在所有竞争方法中表现最佳，尤其在临床挑战性病例上取得了显著提升。

Conclusion: CalcSeg有效解决了单栈LGE-CMR心肌疤痕分割中的空间上下文不足和困难样本学习问题，为临床疤痕评估提供了更鲁棒的解决方案，代码已开源。

Abstract: Myocardial scar segmentation from single-stack late gadolinium-enhanced cardiac magnetic resonance (LGE-CMR) imaging has been a longstanding and clinically important challenge, particularly in the presence of low tissue contrast, diffuse, and small scar regions. These challenges are further intensified by the limited availability of 3D spatial context. This paper presents CalcSeg, a Confidence-aware latent context curriculum learning framework that leverages fused 3D feature representations from single-stack 2D LGE-CMR images for robust scar segmentation. Specifically, we introduce a dynamic semi-supervised curriculum learning strategy that progressively expands training from easier to more challenging scar cases using a learned confidence-aware scoring function. Such a function integrates errors in the predicted scar maps with quantified epistemic uncertainty and scar burden estimation to automatically assess sample difficulty without requiring manual labels. To compensate for the limited spatial context in single-stack acquisitions, we then develop a latent slice-wise self-attention to capture inter-slice dependencies and infer 3D spatial representations from sparse 2D inputs. We evaluate CalcSeg on multi-center clinical LGE-CMR datasets and benchmark against existing scar segmentation networks. Experimental results show that CalcSeg consistently outperforms all competing methods, particularly with substantial improvements on clinically challenging cases. Our code is released on Github.

</details>


### [105] [DreamHand: Repurposing Video Diffusion Models for Occlusion-Robust Egocentric 3D Hand Motion Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20308)
*Yufei Liu,Xixi Wang,Hao Li,Ganlong Zhao,Kaitong Cai,Chengkai Jin,Chunxiao Liu,Jianbo Liu,Siyuan Huang,Xingang Pan,Hongsheng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DreamHand将视频扩散模型（VDM）重塑为确定性几何编码器，通过单次前向传播提取包含被遮挡及出视野手部信息的场景特征，结合双向时空解码器实现离线片段级别的连续双手3D轨迹恢复，在五个自我中心基准上大幅刷新SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我中心视频为具身AI提供可扩展的操纵数据，但现有方法在恢复度量3D手部轨迹时面临严重物体遮挡和频繁出视野间断的挑战。单帧和窗口时序回归器在手部短暂离开画面时失效，而视频扩散模型虽然能力强，却依赖重计算量的随机多步采样，难以实用化。

Method: 提出DreamHand框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 确定性干净潜在编码器（Deterministic Clean-Latent Encoder），将VDM重定位为确定性几何编码器，通过单次前向传播在干净潜在空间中揭示当前观测之外（遮挡及出视野）的场景内容；(2) 双向时空解码器（Bidirectional Spatiotemporal Decoder），从编码特征中解码出连续的双手轨迹，包含度量位置且无需外部检测器。另设计基于射线的相机求解器，支持免测试时相机内参的配置。

Result: 在五个自我中心基准上全面刷新SOTA：在遮挡严重的ARCTIC基准上MPJPE-p降低30%，在HOT3D上降低40%；当评估中纳入出视野手部时，性能提升可达46%-61%。

Conclusion: DreamHand提供了一条从日常人类视频扩展到机器人操纵数据的规模化路径，通过将视频扩散模型转用作确定性几何编码器，在保持轻量高效（单次前向）的同时，显著提升了对遮挡和出视野手部轨迹的恢复能力。

Abstract: Egocentric video offers scalable manipulation data for embodied AI, yet recovering metric 3D hand trajectories remains challenging due to severe object occlusion and frequent out-of-sight gaps. Existing single-frame and windowed temporal regressors fail when hand shortly leaves the frame, while recent video diffusion models (VDMs) rely on heavy, stochastic multi-step sampling as pixel-space renderers. We instead repurpose VDM into a deterministic geometry encoder. A single forward pass over the clean latent exposes scene content beyond current observations, including occluded and out-of-sight hands. We introduce DreamHand, an offline clip-level framework that extracts features via a Deterministic Clean-Latent Encoder and decodes them with a Bidirectional Spatiotemporal Decoder. DreamHand recovers continuous bimanual trajectories with metric placement and no external detector, while a Ray-Based Camera Solver supports a second configuration that needs no test-time camera intrinsics. Across five egocentric benchmarks, DreamHand sets a new state of the art, cutting MPJPE-p by 30% on occlusion-heavy ARCTIC and 40% on HOT3D. These gains reach 46%-61% once out-of-sight hands are included in the evaluation, offering a scalable path from everyday human video to robot manipulation data.

</details>


### [106] [Inter-X++: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Multimodal Human-Human Interaction Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20312)
*Liang Xu,Chengqun Yang,Zili Lin,Xintao Lv,Yichao Yan,Xin Jin,Zhibo Chen,Xiaokang Yang,Wenjun Zeng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Inter-X++，一个大规模、高保真的人-人交互（HHI）基准数据集，包含11,388个交互序列、超过810万帧，配备精细的全身运动和手指关节数据，以及多层面的丰富标注（层级文本描述、交互类别、因果关系、人物关系个性、顶点级接触图和物理约束）。同时提出OpenHHI统一表示与建模框架，联合优化交互重建和语义理解，在生成与感知任务上均达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HHI数据集和建模方法受限于低保真运动学数据、缺乏精细手势、多模态标注稀缺；同时交互表示碎片化、评估协议不一致，阻碍了公平严谨的基准测试，亟需一个系统性解决这些瓶颈的综合基准。

Method: 采用新型混合动作捕捉系统采集高保真数据，提供精细全身运动和手指关节；引入多层面标注（层级文本、交互类别、因果顺序、关系个性、接触图和物理约束）；构建统一的四类下游任务测试平台（涵盖生成与感知两大范式）；标准化交互表示与评估协议；提出OpenHHI统一表示框架，联合优化交互重建与语义理解。

Result: OpenHHI在生成和感知任务上均达到最先进性能，证明统一表示能同时弥合交互理解与生成之间的鸿沟。数据集包含11,388个高保真交互序列和超过810万帧。

Conclusion: Inter-X++提供了全面且标准化的HHI基准，OpenHHI框架验证了统一表示在同时支持交互理解与生成方面的有效性，为智能数字人系统的交互感知与合成奠定了数据与方法基础。

Abstract: The capability to perceive and synthesize human-human interactions is fundamental to developing intelligent digital human systems. However, existing datasets and modeling approaches are fundamentally constrained by low-fidelity kinematics, the omission of dexterous hand gestures and a severe lack of rich multimodal annotations. Furthermore, fragmented interaction representations and inconsistent evaluation protocols also impede fair and rigorous benchmarking. To systematically address these bottlenecks, we present Inter-X++, a comprehensive and large-scale benchmark designed to empower versatile HHI analysis. Captured via a novel hybrid motion capture system, Inter-X++ provides 11,388 high-fidelity interaction sequences and over 8.1M frames, featuring precise whole-body movements and detailed finger articulations. Meanwhile, we enrich the data foundation with multifaceted annotations, including hierarchical fine-grained textual descriptions, interaction categories, causal interaction orders, the relationship and personality of the subjects, as well as vertex-level contact maps and physically regularized constraints. Leveraging these elaborate annotations, we formulate a unified testing ground comprising four categories of downstream tasks that symmetrically span both generative and perceptive paradigms. To eliminate benchmarking ambiguities, we systematically standardize the interaction representations and evaluation protocols. Finally, we go beyond dataset construction to propose OpenHHI, a single and unified HHI representation and modeling framework that jointly optimizes interaction reconstruction and semantic understanding. Extensive experiments reveal that OpenHHI achieves state-of-the-art performance on both generation and perception tasks. This definitively proves that our unified representation successfully bridges interaction understanding and generation simultaneously.

</details>


### [107] [Question-Guided Evidence Acquisition for Multimodal Visual Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19739)
*Alin-Ionut Popa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出Q-Guide，一个轻量级智能体，通过推理时额外的"慢感知"计算来解决多模态LLM在文档问答中视觉感知不足的问题。Q-Guide读取问题、判断缺少的证据、调用针对性的工具（读文本、缩放细节、区域定位），在DocVQA2026和Manga109上显著超越直接提示和现有多智能体系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态LLM在文档视觉问答中常常无法可靠读取文档内容，小文本、表格、视觉线索和拓扑元素在直接视觉推理下容易出错。现有文档VQA系统将感知视为固定的单次快速编码过程，无法在推理时针对问题需求进行更细致的感知调整。作者认为文档VQA需要更慢、更审慎的感知机制，即在推理时消耗额外计算来决定下一步该查看什么。

Method: 提出Q-Guide，一个小型智能体：它读取用户问题，判断当前缺失的证据，并调用针对性工具来获取这些证据——需要文本时读取文本，需要细节时缩放，需要位置时进行区域定位。该智能体支持多轮迭代感知，每轮决定"下一步看什么"，达到知觉预算后再回答。

Result: 在DocVQA2026上Q-Guide达到65.0%，对比直接提示的40.0%；在Manga109上达到32.4%，对比24.4%。超过近期多智能体文档系统，且在三个Claude骨干（Opus 4.6、Sonnet 4.6、Opus 4.5）上均表现稳定。精度随感知预算（轮次）提升，两到三轮内获得大部分增益。增益来自将感知引导到正确位置，而非复杂控制逻辑——添加规划器、路由器或多智能体协作并无帮助。

Conclusion: 文档VQA应引入推理时的主动感知机制而非固定的单次编码。简单的"问-查-答"智能体循环足以产生显著提升，复杂控制架构并非必要。感知预算与精度成正比，且收益主要源于感知方向的选择而非控制逻辑的复杂度。

Abstract: Multimodal LLMs can see a document, but they often can't read it reliably. Small text, tables, visual cues, and topological elements still trip them up under direct visual inference, even when the page is already sitting in the model's context. Most document-VQA systems treat perception as fixed: they encode the page once, ask the question, and answer from whatever the model happened to extract in that single fast pass. We think document VQA needs slower, more deliberate perception: rather than answering from one fixed encoding, the model should spend a bit of extra compute at inference time working out what to look at next, and only then answer. We build this into \textbf{Q-Guide}, a small agent that reads a question, works out what evidence it is still missing, and calls targeted tool(s) to recover it---reading text where text is needed, zooming in where detail is needed, or grounding a region where position matters. On DocVQA2026 and Manga109, Q-Guide outperforms both direct prompting and recent multi-agent document systems ($65.0\%$ vs.\ $40.0\%$ on DocVQA2026, $32.4\%$ vs.\ $24.4\%$ on Manga109), and the improvement holds across three Claude backbones (Opus 4.6, Sonnet 4.6, and Opus 4.5). We find that accuracy scales with the perception budget---most of the gain appears within two to three deliberate rounds---and that the gain comes from directing perception to the right place, not from complex control logic: adding planners, routers, or multiple collaborating agents does not help.

</details>


### [108] [Swift-Image: Exploring the Performance Frontier of Compact Unified Image Generation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20334)
*Taihang Hu,Zhao Wang,Zuan Gao,Tao Liu,Hao Yan,Zhengze Xu,Yuhang Yu,Yongchao Du,Xingjian Wang,Jun Zheng,Qinye Zhou,Zhengrui Chen,Chao Lin,Yefeng Shen,Zhengtao Wu,Ge Wu,Xiaoli Xu,Denghui Yang,Huayu Zhang,Mingzhou Zhang,Mengting Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Swift-Image 是一个紧凑统一的文本到图像生成、单图编辑和多图编辑模型。通过系统的训练工程（渐进式训练流水线、并行专家强化学习、多教师策略蒸馏、Prompt Enhancer、结构剪枝和少步蒸馏），仅用 6B 参数和 243K GPU 训练小时即在开源模型中取得领先的聚合性能，3B 压缩模型几乎无性能损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在有限计算预算下，通过系统的训练工程能将一个相对较小的视觉生成器（6B 单流 DiT）推向多强的性能水平，同时解决异构目标（生成与编辑）之间的干扰问题，并实现高效部署。

Method: 1) 采用 6B 单流 DiT 作为高效骨干；2) 渐进式训练流水线，从广泛语义覆盖演进到更高分辨率、更强视觉质量和统一生成-编辑监督；3) 后训练阶段使用并行专家强化学习与多教师在线策略蒸馏以缓解异构目标干扰；4) 引入 Prompt Enhancer 将高层推理与像素级渲染解耦；5) 通过结构剪枝与少步蒸馏产出 3B 和加速变体。

Result: Swift-Image 在 6B 参数和 243K GPU 训练小时下取得开源模型中的领先聚合性能；压缩到 3B 后几乎无性能损失；少步蒸馏在显著减少采样步数的同时进一步提升了聚合编辑性能。

Conclusion: 研究表明通过精心设计的训练工程（架构、数据课程、后训练、提示增强与模型压缩）可在有限算力下让紧凑模型达到领先水平，并总结了实用的工程经验与教训。

Abstract: We present Swift-Image, a compact unified model for text-to-image generation, single-image editing, and multi-image editing. Our goal is to explore how far a relatively small visual generator can be pushed through systematic training engineering under a constrained computational budget. Swift-Image adopts an efficient 6B single-stream DiT and a progressive training pipeline that evolves from broad semantic coverage to higher resolution, stronger visual quality, and unified generation-editing supervision. For post-training, we employ parallel expert reinforcement learning followed by multi-teacher on-policy distillation to alleviate interference among heterogeneous objectives. We further decouple high-level reasoning from pixel-level rendering with a Prompt Enhancer that translates user requests into generator-aligned visual specifications. For efficient deployment, structural pruning and few-step distillation produce 3B and accelerated variants. Swift-Image achieves leading aggregate performance among evaluated open-source models with only 6B parameters and 243K GPU training hours; the compressed 3B model incurs nearly no loss, while few-step distillation further improves aggregate editing performance with substantially fewer sampling steps. Our study also summarizes practical lessons for architecture, data curriculum, post-training, prompt enhancement, and model compression.

</details>


### [109] [4DAnyone: Create Anyone in 4D from a Casual Monocular Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20335)
*Yudong Jin,Tao Xie,Qihang Zhang,Zehong Shen,Zhen Xu,Yujun Shen,Hujun Bao,Xiaowei Zhou,Yinghao Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 4DAnyone是一个从无标定单目视频中重建4D人类的方法，通过生成重建级多视角一致视频并将其提升为4D Gaussian Splatting（4DGS）。它识别出大规模视图合成中的有界注意力上下文问题，提出参考上下文打包（RCP）和目标上下文路由（TCR）两种设计解决参考上下文与目标上下文的瓶颈，并构建了MVGameHuman数据集进行训练。实验表明其在新视角视频质量和下游4DGS重建上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有相机控制的视频扩散模型在合成新视角视频时，当扩展到4DGS重建所需的数十个目标视角时无法维持一致性。作者将该失败归因于有界注意力上下文问题：当目标视角数量超过单个DiT前向传播容量时必须分组处理，产生两个耦合瓶颈——参考上下文侧，条件化所有先前生成视图导致O(N)增长，削弱跨视角外观指导；目标上下文侧，不相连的组无法直接交换信息，造成全局结构漂移。

Method: 提出两个互补设计：1) 参考上下文打包（RCP）将增长的参考视图压缩为固定长度的混合分辨率上下文，实现O(1)参考上下文复杂度；2) 目标上下文路由（TCR）在去噪过程中轮换目标视角分组，在高噪声步共享组间上下文，在低噪声步稳定细节。此外利用自研游戏引擎构建MVGameHuman数据集，并结合光stage与野外视频数据集进行训练。

Result: 在DNA-Rendering和DyMVHumans数据集上，4DAnyone在新视角视频质量和下游4DGS重建方面均优于现有方法，并展现鲁棒的野外泛化能力。

Conclusion: 4DAnyone通过解决有界注意力上下文问题（RCP与TCR），实现了从单目无标定视频到4DGS的高质量4D人类重建，在多个基准上取得state-of-the-art性能，具备良好的野外泛化能力。

Abstract: We present 4DAnyone, a framework for reconstructing 4D humans from an uncalibrated monocular video by generating reconstruction-grade multiview-consistent videos and lifting them into 4D Gaussian Splatting (4DGS). Existing camera-controlled video diffusion models synthesize plausible novel-view videos but fail to maintain consistency when scaled to the tens of target views required for 4DGS reconstruction. We identify this failure as a bounded-attention-context problem: when target views exceed the capacity of a single DiT forward pass, they must be split into groups, exposing two coupled bottlenecks. On the reference-context side, conditioning on all previously generated views grows as $O(N)$, weakening cross-view appearance guidance. On the target-context side, disjoint groups cannot directly exchange information, causing global structural drift. 4DAnyone addresses both bottlenecks with two complementary designs: Reference Context Packing (RCP) compresses growing reference views into a fixed-length mixed-resolution context with $O(1)$ reference-context complexity, while Target Context Routing (TCR) rotates target-view groupings during denoising to share context across groups at high-noise steps and stabilize details at low-noise steps. We further build the MVGameHuman dataset using our in-house game engine and combine it with light-stage and in-the-wild video datasets for training. Experiments on DNA-Rendering and DyMVHumans show that 4DAnyone outperforms prior methods in both novel-view video quality and downstream 4DGS reconstruction, with robust in-the-wild generalization. See our project page for video results and source code: https://4danyone.github.io.

</details>


### [110] [WithEveryone: Unified Planning and Identity Grounding for Group Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20336)
*Hengyuan Xu,Qixun Wang,Yiji Cheng,Miles Yang,Zhao Zhong,Wei Cheng,Xingjun Ma,Yu-gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出WithEveryone框架，用于生成包含多达十个参考身份人物的群体图像，通过身份—布局规划和布局锚定的ID损失实现高保真度的多重身份保留生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当场景中需要包含多个指定人物时，身份保留的图像生成变得不可靠。模型不仅需要保留每个身份，还要将每个参考身份绑定到特定的人物和位置，同时训练时的身份损失需要在多个噪声预测人脸之间建立对应关系，这构成了一个多身份绑定的挑战。

Method: WithEveryone采用三个核心设计：(1) 将每个选择身份作为寻址token注入；(2) 预测结构化的身份—布局计划并将其渲染为视觉条件；(3) 提出Layout-Grounded ID Loss，利用标注的人脸区域直接监督目标身份，避免不稳定的基于嵌入的人脸匹配；(4) ID Representation Forcing在每个身份的图像合成前进行预测训练。

Result: 在身份不相交的基准测试上，WithEveryone实现了最高的目标上下文身份相似度，将人脸相似度从GPT-Image-2的0.462提升到0.499，同时将复制粘贴伪影从0.169降低到0.055。覆盖率高达97.3%，重复率仅为2.8%。

Conclusion: 显式的身份—布局锚定能够使身份保留生成扩展到更大的群体，而无需依赖直接的参考人脸复制。

Abstract: Identity-preserving image generation becomes increasingly unreliable when a scene must contain many specified people. Beyond retaining each identity, the model must bind every reference to a distinct person and location, while training-time identity losses must establish correspondence among several noisy predicted faces. We introduce WithEveryone, a unified framework for generating group images up to ten reference identities. WithEveryone injects each selected identity as an addressed token, predicts a structured identity--layout plan, and renders the plan as a visual condition. Its key objective, Layout-Grounded ID Loss, uses annotated face regions to supervise the intended identities directly, avoiding unstable embedding-based face matching; ID Representation Forcing additionally trains a prediction for each identity before image synthesis. On an identity-disjoint benchmark, WithEveryone achieves the highest target-context identity similarity, improving face similarity from 0.462 for GPT-Image-2 to 0.499, while reducing copy-paste artifacts from 0.169 to 0.055. It further covers 97.3\% of the requested identities with a duplicate rate of only 2.8\%. These results show that explicit identity--layout grounding enables identity-preserving generation to scale to larger groups without relying on direct reference-face copying.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [111] [Incident-Data Robustness Analysis of the OWASP Top 10 for LLM Applications (2026): How a Community-Expert Ranking Holds Up Against a Large-Scale LLM Incident Corpus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19266)
*Kyriakos "Rock" Lambros,Steve Wilson*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对OWASP Top 10 LLM应用风险排名，构建了包含7,714个快照、6,639个已标注的LLM安全事件语料库，采用贝叶斯测量误差模型校正分类器精度/召回率后得出基于事件的排名，并与专家投票排名（权重0.75专家/0.25数据）进行对比。两者一致性极弱（Cohen's κ≈0.20，90%区间跨越零），但专家排名依然稳健；四项前沿分类器预注册对比中无一胜出，均未超过事件发生率基线的平衡准确率0.863，地面真值检验维持了基线排序（Spearman ρ=0.918）。作者声明这是两位工作组成员的探索性分析，非官方OWASP发布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OWASP Top 10是由安全从业者社区评判的专家排名，但作者质疑该专家共识是否与现实安全事件数据一致，旨在用大规模真实事件语料检验专家排名的数据基础。

Method: 构建大规模LLM安全事件语料库（来自CVE、GHSA、OSV、AIAAIC），使用20项分类法标注6,639个事件；采用贝叶斯测量误差模型校正各分类计数中的分类器精确率和召回率，得出基于事件的排名；将专家投票与数据信号按0.75:0.25固定权重混合生成2026候选清单；并用Cohen's κ衡量两排名一致性，通过预注册的四个前沿分类器bake-off及地面真值检验验证稳健性。

Result: 专家排名与事件数据排名一致性极弱（Cohen's κ≈0.20，90%置信区间跨越零）；预注册的四个前沿分类器 bake-off 中无胜出者，均未超过事件发生率基线的平衡准确率0.863；地面真值检验确认基线排序的Spearman ρ=0.918，说明事件发生率基线排序稳健。

Conclusion: 专家排名与真实事件数据存在显著分歧，但专家排名仍然稳健；该研究为探索性分析，不替代官方OWASP排名或流程。建议在保留专家共识的同时，以数据驱动信号作为补充修正手段。

Abstract: The OWASP Top 10 for LLM Applications ranks the risks that a community of security practitioners judges most important. We ask a narrower question: checked against the record of real incidents, does that expert ranking agree with the data? We assembled a large-scale corpus of LLM-security incidents (7,714 snapshotted and 6,639 labeled against the 20-entry taxonomy) drawn from CVE, GHSA, OSV, and AIAAIC, and derived an incident-based ranking with a Bayesian measurement-error model that corrects each category's count for classifier precision and recall. The 2026 candidate list blends the two signals at fixed weights, 0.75 on the expert vote and 0.25 on the data, so the corpus corrects the consensus without overturning it. The agreement between the two rankings is weak: Cohen's $κ\approx 0.20$, with a 90% interval that crosses zero. The expert ranking is nonetheless robust. A pre-registered bake-off of four frontier classifiers returns no winner. None beats the incidence floor's balanced accuracy of 0.863. A ground-truth check leaves the floor's ordering (Spearman $ρ= 0.918$ against held-out truth) in place. This is an exploratory analysis by two working-group members, not the official OWASP release, and it does not supersede the official list or process.

</details>


### [112] [ABEAT: Efficient and Anonymous Encryption for ABE-based Dynamic Group Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19302)
*Hongmiao Yu,Silas Richelson,Jiachen Chen,K. K. Ramakrishnan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出ABEAT，一个高效且匿名的动态群组通信系统。它基于属性基加密（ABE）和图命名空间，引入新的匿名KP-ABE方案隐藏密文中的属性以保护接收者匿名性，同时将非目标接收者的解密开销降低超过90倍（相比HVE），且比无匿名性的FABEO还低40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态群组通信需要灵活高效的多对多通信，并需保持消息接收者的匿名性（如应急响应场景）。现有的ABE方案虽然支持群组通信，但无法在高效通信的同时保护接收者匿名性，且非目标接收者的验证/解密开销过高。

Method: ABEAT系统结合属性基加密（ABE）与基于图的命名空间，提出一种新的匿名KP-ABE方案：在密文中隐藏明文属性以维护接收者匿名性，同时能够防御多种试图破坏匿名性的攻击，并提供快速的接收者验证机制。

Result: ABEAT将非目标接收者的解密开销降低超过当前最先进方案（如HVE）的90倍以上，甚至比不提供匿名性的FABEO方案还低40%。

Conclusion: ABEAT在提供强接收者匿名性的同时，实现了远优于现有方案的效率，特别大幅降低了非目标接收者的开销，为动态群组安全通信提供了实用且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Confidential communication among a dynamic group of participants that ensures flexible and efficient many-to- many communication is highly desired capability. We leverage attribute-based encryption (ABE) for confidential group communication and enhance it by a graph-based namespace to create an efficient framework that allows groups to be formed and changed dynamically. In this paper, we focus on the important additional need to maintain the anonymity of recipients of a message, when using ABE for group communication for a variety of usage scenarios (e.g., emergency response).
  We propose ABEAT, an efficient and anonymous dynamic group communication system that also minimizes overhead on receivers who are not the intended recipients of a message. In ABEAT, we propose a new anonymous KP-ABE approach to maintain recipient anonymity. ABEAT hides the clear attribute in the ciphertext of KP-ABE, and also prevents several attacks that seek to break anonymity. ABEAT provides fast recipient verification, dramatically lowering the decryption overhead for non-recipients by more than a factor of 90 versus the current state of the art such as hidden vector encryption (HVE). In fact, it is even 40% less than FABEO, which offers no anonymity.

</details>


### [113] [Aray: Deterministic-First Synthesis of Benign Artifacts for YARA Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19387)
*Emanuel C. A. Valente,Lourenço A. P. Júnior,Leonardo Gonçalves Chahud,Júlio Cezar Estrella,Marcus Botacin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Aray，一个确定性优先的YARA解释器与正向测试样本（fixture）合成器。它能在不重现恶意软件行为、也不依赖模型直接生成源码或二进制结构的前提下，从YARA规则自动构造一个能触发该规则正例匹配的合法文件。在416个公开规则条目上实现了97.6%的整体成功率（406/416），且所有可构造性预检通过的规则均成功匹配其原始规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: YARA规则易于分发，但用于演示正例匹配的恶意软件样本却难以传播，这给存储、持续集成、灾难恢复演练和可复现的扫描器验证带来困难。构造替代fixture不能简单嵌入字面量，因为YARA条件可组合备选、计数、偏移、整数读取和可执行容器约束，同时生成的文件不应重现恶意行为。正例验证是存在性问题，只需规则匹配集中的某个文件成员，无需重建原始样本。

Method: Aray采用确定性优先的YARA解释器架构。模型可以提出构造性归一化或类型化提取回退方案，但绝不接触后端源码或二进制结构。常规代码负责：验证归一化规则、推导字符串与整数见证、执行提取、路由、碰撞检查布局以及ELF、PE或通用序列化。只有剩余的归一化语义才交给有界的模型判定器。训练流程包含归一化（无模型辅助时182条，模型归一化后234条）、可构造性预检（406条）及原始规则oracle验证。

Result: 在416个公开规则条目上，归一化阶段无模型帮助接受182条，模型归一化后接受234条。可构造性预检接纳406条，所有预检通过的fixture均能匹配其上流原始规则，实现了406/416（97.6%）的整体成功率，以及406/406（100%）的可构造规则成功率。十个预期预检处置，无扫描器不匹配或构造失败；一个不可达端点确认实现阶段零模型调用。

Conclusion: 原始规则oracle验证了生成的fixture能匹配其源规则；但要证明对所有可能文件的蕴含关系，是另一个更强的独立目标。两个锚定正则表达式失败在最终运行前已修复，因此这些结果是修复后的系统指标，而非留出的数据集估计。

Abstract: A YARA rule is easy to distribute, but the malware sample used to demonstrate a positive match is not. This complicates storage, continuous integration, disaster-recovery exercises, and reproducible scanner validation. Constructing a replacement fixture requires more than embedding literals: YARA conditions can combine alternatives, counts, offsets, integer reads, and executable-container constraints, while the resulting file should not reproduce malware behavior. Positive validation is existential: it requires one file-level member of a rule's match set, not reconstruction of the originating sample. We present Aray, a deterministic-first YARA interpreter and positive-fixture synthesizer. Models may propose constructive normalizations or typed extraction fallbacks, but never backend source or binary structure. Conventional code validates normalized rules, derives string and integer witnesses, and performs extraction, routing, collision-checked layout, and ELF, PE, or generic serialization. Only residual normalization semantics reach a bounded model judge. We evaluated Aray over 416 public-rule entries. Normalization accepted 182 entries without model assistance and 234 after model normalization. Constructibility preflight admitted 406 entries, and every admitted fixture matched its upstream original rule. This yields 406/416 (97.6%) overall and 406/406 among constructible rules, with ten expected preflight dispositions and no scanner mismatches or construction failures. An unreachable endpoint confirmed zero model invocations during realization. The original-rule oracle validates generated fixtures against their source rules; proving implication for all possible files is a separate, stronger objective. Two anchored-regex failures were repaired before the final run, so these are post-fix systems results, not a held-out estimate.

</details>


### [114] [Redactable blockchains and polynomial equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19401)
*Alexander Demin,Alexey Ovchinnikov,Vladimir Shpilrain*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文为可编辑认证数据结构（RADS）开发了新的构建工具，其安全性基于后量子假设——即求解多元多项式方程组的一维/多维求逆问题，该问题目前被认为不存在高效的量子求解算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着量子计算的发展，传统的基于数论难题（如大整数分解、离散对数）的认证数据结构面临安全威胁，需要开发具有后量子安全性的可编辑认证数据结构。

Method: 文中提出了新的单向函数，其求逆等价于求解一个或多个变量的多项式方程（或多项式方程组）。通过合理选择参数，该问题目前被认为在量子计算下是安全的。

Result: 成功构造了具有后量子安全性的可编辑认证数据结构，利用多元多项式方程组求逆的困难性作为安全基础。

Conclusion: 多元多项式方程求解可作为后量子安全的密码学基础，为可编辑认证数据结构提供了新的安全构建工具。

Abstract: We develop new tools for constructing redactable authenticated data structures with post-quantum security. In our construction, inverting the proposed one-way function means solving a polynomial equation (or a system of polynomial equations) in more than one variable. This is presently considered quantum-safe, i.e., there is no known quantum algorithm that could solve this problem efficiently if parameters are chosen wisely.

</details>


### [115] [A Federated Learning Framework for Privacy-Preserving Oral Cancer Screening on Smartphones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19462)
*Lena D. Swamikannan,Akshay Bhagwan Sonawane,Jay S. Patel,C. S. Mani,Lakshmi Narayana,Lakshman Tamil*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个实用的联邦学习框架，通过Tailscale安全组网和Flower框架的服务器端聚合，支持跨地理分布的AI医疗研究人员协作开发口腔癌筛查模型。在手机端轻量级模型中，基于MobileNetV4-Conv-Small的全局模型（GM-V4）经FedAvg聚合后性能最佳，AUC达0.929，准确率87%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学领域数据获取受法规和患者隐私限制，特别是临床口腔图像尤为稀缺。联邦学习可在不集中或共享患者数据的前提下实现跨分散数据集的协作模型开发，解决医疗AI数据可用性瓶颈。

Method: 搭建实用联邦学习框架：使用Tailscale实现客户端间安全组网和实时通信；采用Flower框架进行服务器端聚合；客户端部署和编排为手动配置，不使用企业级FL平台；评估移动端友好的轻量级架构（MobileNetV2、MobileNetV3Large、MobileNetV4-Conv-Small），使用FedAvg进行全局模型聚合。

Result: 在所有通过FedAvg聚合的轻量级全局模型中，基于MobileNetV4-Conv-Small的全局模型GM-V4性能最佳，达到AUC 0.929和87%的准确率。

Conclusion: 所提出的联邦学习框架能够有效支持口腔癌筛查模型的跨地域协作开发，且轻量级MobileNetV4架构在移动端筛查应用中展现出最优性能，证明了在不共享患者数据的前提下构建鲁棒医疗AI模型的可行性。

Abstract: Data are the cornerstone of robust AI models. However, in the medical domain, access to reliable data is constrained by regulatory requirements and patient privacy, and clinical oral images are particularly difficult to obtain. Federated learning (FL) mitigates these constraints by enabling collaborative model development across decentralized datasets without centralizing or sharing patient data.
  This work presents a practical FL framework that supports geographically distributed collaboration among AI healthcare researchers and facilitates the development of robust models for oral cancer screening. Client devices were interconnected via Tailscale to provide secure networking and real-time communication. We implemented the FL workflow using the Flower framework for server-side aggregation, while client deployment and orchestration were configured manually; no enterprise FL platforms were used. To support a smartphone-based screening application, we evaluated lightweight, mobile-friendly architectures including MobileNetV2, MobileNetV3Large, and MobileNetV4-Conv-Small (MNv4-Conv-S). Across the global lightweight models aggregated using FedAvg, the MNv4-Conv-S based global model (GM-V4) achieved the best performance, reaching an AUC of 0.929 and an accuracy of 87%

</details>


### [116] [AEGIS: Attention-Embedding Gradient Isolation Shield - Triple-Channel Gradient Masking for Privacy-Preserving Federated LLM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19534)
*Ye Tao,Hong Shen,Hui Tian,Xin Wang,Can Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出AEGIS（注意力-嵌入梯度隔离盾），一种轻量级防御方法，通过三种后向路径操作同时封堵联邦学习中梯度反演攻击利用的所有三个信息泄漏通道，在不改变架构的情况下将令牌恢复率降至接近零，同时保持或提升模型效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中，梯度反演攻击能够从共享梯度中恢复私有训练文本，严重威胁协作模型训练的安全。现有防御方法最多只能解决一个泄漏通道，且要么降低模型效用，要么遗留结构信号，缺乏一种全面的防御方案来封堵所有分析性泄漏路径。

Method: AEGIS通过三种后向路径操作封堵三个泄漏通道：(1) 冻结注意力投影参数，从构造上消除通道1（注意力输出投影梯度中的低秩子空间）；(2) 向嵌入梯度注入校准噪声，破坏通道2（行范数稀疏性揭示令牌存在性）；(3) 向MLP扩展梯度注入逐块噪声，掩蔽通道3（可恢复子空间信号）。所有掩蔽在同一梯度上完成，同时用于本地优化器更新和服务器导出，确保两侧均无干净信号。

Result: 在11个模型和6个数据集上的评估表明，AEGIS能将针对分析型和基于优化的梯度反演攻击的令牌恢复率降至接近零，同时保持或提升模型效用。对通道1和2提供了形式化保证，并对完全了解机制的适应性对手进行了经验验证。

Conclusion: AEGIS是一种全面的轻量级防御方案，无需架构变更即可同时封堵全部三个梯度泄漏通道，为联邦学习中的隐私保护提供了有效且可验证的解决方案。

Abstract: Gradient inversion attacks recover private training text from gradients shared in federated learning, posing a serious threat to collaborative model training. Through our analysis of transformer gradient structure, we identify three channels through which private token information leaks: the attention output projection gradient exposes a low-rank subspace that encodes input embeddings (Channel 1), the embedding gradient's row-norm sparsity directly reveals which tokens are present (Channel 2), and the MLP expansion gradient carries a recoverable subspace signal analogous to Channel 1 (Channel 3). State-of-the-art attacks exploit these channels analytically to achieve near-exact token recovery in seconds. Existing defences address at most one channel and either degrade model utility or leave the remaining structural signals intact.
  We introduce AEGIS (Attention-Embedding Gradient Isolation Shield), a lightweight defence that closes all three analytical channels with three backward-path operations requiring no architectural changes: freezing attention projection parameters eliminates Channel 1 by construction, calibrated noise injection into the embedding gradient destroys Channel 2's token-presence signal, and analogous per-block noise injection into the MLP expansion gradient masks Channel 3. The same masked gradient drives both the local optimiser step and the server export, so no clean signal is retained on either side.
  Evaluated across 11 models and six datasets, AEGIS reduces token recovery rates to near zero against a range of gradient inversion attacks, both analytical and optimisation-based, while preserving or improving model utility. We provide formal guarantees for Channels 1 and 2 and validate the full defence empirically against adaptive adversaries with complete knowledge of the mechanism.

</details>


### [117] [Enhancing Privacy in Federated Learning via Dual Obfuscation of Gradients and Training Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19650)
*Yuki Itabashi,Hiroto Sawada,Mare Hirose,Shoko Imaizumi,Hitoshi Kiya*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种双混淆方法，通过联合混淆更新信息和训练图像来增强联邦学习对图像还原攻击的鲁棒性。该方法结合基于随机二进制权重的梯度稀疏化技术与图像加密技术，二者互补：前者减少攻击者可获得的原始梯度信息量，后者降低重建图像的视觉可解释性。在ViT图像分类任务上的实验表明，该方法在评估设置下减少了APRIL攻击恢复的视觉信息，且未造成超出图像加密本身的额外分类性能退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习允许客户端在本地保留数据的同时协作训练模型，但近期研究表明可以通过共享的模型更新重建训练数据，这构成了严重的隐私泄露威胁，亟需有效的防御方法。

Method: 提出双重混淆技术：1) 基于随机二进制权重的鲁棒性增强技术，将部分梯度元素随机置零以减少攻击者可获得的梯度信息量；2) 图像加密技术，降低重建图像的视觉可解释性。图像加密支持每个客户端和每张图像使用独立密钥，避免了显式密钥共享。

Result: 在基于Vision Transformer的图像分类任务上，所提方法在评估设置下减少了APRIL攻击恢复的视觉信息，且没有造成超出图像加密本身额外的分类性能下降。

Conclusion: 尽管该组合不提供绝对的安全保证，但实验结果证明了将梯度修改与图像加密相结合用于隐私增强联邦学习的潜在价值。

Abstract: Federated learning enables collaborative model training while keeping data locally at each client; however, recent studies have shown that training data can be reconstructed from shared model updates. To address this issue, this paper proposes a dual obfuscation method that enhances robustness against image restoration attacks by jointly obfuscating updated information and training images. The proposed method combines a robustness enhancement technique based on random binary weights, which randomly sets a portion of gradient elements to zero, with an image encryption technique. These techniques provide complementary protection by reducing the amount of original gradient information available to an attacker and the visual interpretability of reconstructed images, respectively. Furthermore, the image encryption technique allows independent keys to be used for each client and each image, avoiding explicit key sharing. Experimental results on an image classification task using a Vision Transformer (ViT) show that the proposed method reduces the visual information recovered by Attention Privacy Leakage (APRIL) under the evaluated settings without causing additional degradation in classification performance beyond that caused by image encryption. Although the proposed combination does not provide an absolute security guarantee, the results demonstrate the potential benefit of combining gradient modification and image encryption for privacy-enhanced federated learning.

</details>


### [118] [TGL-APT: Temporal Graph Learning with Graph Distillation for Efficient APT Investigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19750)
*Jing Chen,Ayong Ye,Yuanhuang Liu,Yuexin Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TGL-APT是一个基于信息瓶颈引导的自适应APT调查框架，通过图蒸馏抑制溯源图中冗余信息，结合自适应时序图学习和跨时空攻击指纹对齐，在三个DARPA E3数据集上达到95.7%、90.9%和88.9%的F1分数，同时相比KAIROS将训练时间、检测延迟和内存占用分别降低约39%、33%和22%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: APT攻击具有隐蔽性和多阶段特性，传统的溯源图全图学习方法面临计算开销大和长攻击序列关联困难的问题——攻击相关证据稀疏且埋藏在大量常规系统活动中，需要一种能够聚焦关键信息同时保持高效检测的方法。

Method: TGL-APT包含三个互补组件：(1) 信息瓶颈引导的图蒸馏，抑制溯源冗余同时约束结构失真并保留因果可达性；(2) 自适应时序图学习，随节点相关性演化持续精炼核心节点集；(3) 跨时空攻击指纹对齐，在不同实体和时间窗口间关联碎片化可疑活动。最后通过因果扩展和阶段刻画重建完整攻击过程。

Result: 在三个DARPA E3数据集上F1分数分别达到95.7%、90.9%和88.9%；相比KAIROS，训练时间降低约39%，检测延迟降低约33%，内存占用降低约22%。

Conclusion: TGL-APT有效平衡了检测性能、计算效率和调查能力，验证了攻击相关信息非均匀分布且由信息瓶颈节点中介的观察在实际APT分析中的有效性。

Abstract: Advanced Persistent Threat (APT) attacks pose a critical challenge to modern systems, as their stealthy, multi-stage nature renders conventional detection methods ineffective. While provenance graphs provide rich behavioral context for attack investigation, attack-relevant evidence is often sparse and embedded in large volumes of routine system activity, making full-graph learning both computationally expensive and difficult to correlate over long attack sequences. We present TGL-APT, an adaptive investigation framework built on the observation that attack-relevant information is non-uniformly distributed and often mediated by structurally influential or behaviorally distinctive entities, which we characterize as information-bottleneck nodes. TGL-APT combines three complementary components: (1) information-bottleneck-guided graph distillation that suppresses provenance redundancy while bounding structural distortion and preserving causal reachability; (2) adaptive temporal graph learning that continuously refines the core node set as node relevance evolves; and (3) cross-spatiotemporal attack fingerprint alignment that associates fragmented suspicious activities across different entities and time windows. Finally, causal expansion and stage characterization reconstruct coherent attack processes for investigation. Experiments on three DARPA E3 datasets show F1-scores of 95.7%, 90.9%, and 88.9%, while reducing training time, detection latency, and memory usage by approximately 39%, 33%, and 22%, respectively, compared with KAIROS. These results demonstrate that TGL-APT effectively balances detection performance, computational efficiency, and investigation capability for provenance-based APT analysis.

</details>


### [119] [Survival of~the~Stealthiest: Evolving Low-Entropy Ransomware via~Genetic Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19821)
*Efrat Levenberg,Kristina Sviazhina,Ayelet Butman,Pierre Parrend,Harel Berger*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出将勒索软件部署重构为基于搜索的软件工程优化问题，利用遗传算法在系统行为基线统计偏差的硬约束下优化数据加密，使攻击模式能够规避行为监控器的指纹检测，实现数小时而非数分钟的隐蔽持久攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统勒索软件部署采用大规模加密过程，容易被现代防御系统立即检测。现代威胁中存在持续性差距——攻击者希望攻击能在数小时而非数分钟内保持隐蔽，这需要范式转变。

Method: 将勒索软件执行框架为基于搜索的软件工程（SBSE）优化问题，使用遗传算法（GA）在系统活动统计偏差硬约束下优化数据加密策略，以生成能够规避行为监控器指纹检测技术的进化攻击模式。

Result: 实验表明进化出的攻击模式能够规避行为监控器的指纹检测技术，证明了基于搜索的方法能够生成具有隐蔽性的恶意软件攻击模式。

Conclusion: 基于搜索的方法为生成规避性恶意软件提供了强大框架，突显了自动化软件防御面临的新兴挑战。

Abstract: Traditional ransomware deployment often relies on massive encryption procedure, triggering immediate detection by modern defense systems. This work introduces a paradigm shift in cryptographic attacks by framing ransomware execution as a Search-Based Software Engineering (SBSE) optimization problem. This approach addresses the persistence gap observed in modern threats, where attacks aim to remain undercover for hours rather than minutes. Using a Genetic Algorithm (GA), we optimize data encryption under a hard constraint on the statistical deviation from baseline system activity. We demonstrate that our evolved attack patterns can evade behavioral monitors under fingerprinting techniques. Our results suggest that search-based methods provide a powerful framework for generating evasive malware, highlighting an emerging challenge for automated software defense.

</details>


### [120] [MaliciousSkillBench: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Malicious Agent Skill Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19901)
*Yue Wang,Yi Liu,Gelei Deng,Ying Zhang,Yuekang Li,Zhenyu Chen,Leo Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出MaliciousSkillBench基准，整合13个公开来源构建恶意Agent Skill检测基准（9,740个Skill，含7,505个恶意和2,235个良性），并评估多种检测方法，发现现有检测器在跨源（Source-Disjoint）评估下性能大幅下降，揭示可靠检测需要更广的跨源覆盖和联合评估攻击检测与良性误报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent Skills为LLM智能体扩展了可复用的指令包（可含脚本、资源和服务配置），这为恶意行为提供了直接的分发渠道。然而现有恶意Skill数据集在来源、工件格式、证据体系和良性样本覆盖上分散割裂，且存在重复和结构相关内容，难以直接聚合和评估，亟需一个统一的综合基准。

Method: 整合13个公开来源（其中11个贡献核心恶意工件），将8,414条原始恶意记录规范化归约至7,539条归一化唯一身份，组成4,588个可操作的结构族；经保守交叉标签冲突排除后，主基准含9,740个Skills（7,505恶意、2,235良性）。将4,983个恶意身份统一映射到11个攻击类别以刻画覆盖范围，并评估三种学习型文本检测器（含TF-IDF SVM、随机宏F1最佳达0.932）和三种现成Skill扫描器。

Result: 学习型检测器在随机划分下达到0.882-0.932 Random Macro-F1，但在Source-Disjoint评估下仅0.653-0.665；最强TF-IDF SVM在Random/结构不重叠/Source-Disjoint分别得分0.932/0.916/0.665，保留95.6%恶意召回但产生62.4%良性误报率。现成扫描器在不同但同样不令人满意的操作区间，降低误报以大幅牺牲恶意召回为代价。

Conclusion: 可靠的恶意Skill检测既需要更广泛的跨源基准覆盖，也需要联合衡量攻击检测与良性过度标记的评估方式；现有检测方法在跨源泛化上存在显著不足。

Abstract: Agent Skills extend LLM agents with reusable instruction packages that may also include scripts, resources, and service configuration. This creates a direct distribution channel for malicious behavior, yet existing malicious-Skill datasets are fragmented across sources, artifact formats, evidence regimes, and benign coverage; duplicated and structurally related content further complicates direct aggregation and evaluation. We present MaliciousSkillBench, a comprehensive benchmark for malicious Agent Skill detection. We consolidate 13 public sources, 11 of which contribute Core malicious artifacts, and reduce 8,414 raw malicious records to 7,539 normalized-unique identities in 4,588 operational structural families. After conservative cross-label conflict exclusion, the primary benchmark contains 9,740 Skills: 7,505 malicious and 2,235 benign. To characterize its coverage, we harmonize 11 attack categories for 4,983 malicious identities with supported source-native mappings and find substantial differences in threat composition across sources. We then evaluate three learned text detectors and three off-the-shelf Skill scanners. Learned detectors achieve 0.882-0.932 Random Macro-F1 but only 0.653-0.665 under Source-Disjoint evaluation; the strongest word TF-IDF SVM scores 0.932/0.916/0.665 on Random/structural-disjoint/Source-Disjoint while retaining 95.6% malicious recall but producing 62.4% benign FPR on held-out sources. Off-the-shelf scanners occupy different but also unsatisfactory operating regimes, reducing false positives only at the cost of sharply lower malicious recall. Together, these results show that reliable malicious-Skill detection requires both broader cross-source benchmark coverage and evaluation that jointly measures attack detection and benign over-flagging.

</details>


### [121] [Securing Filesystems for Confidential Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19924)
*Dimitra Giantsidi,Antoine Delignat-Lavaud,Cédric Fournet,Jinnan Guo,Heidi Howard,Tianjiao Huang,Kapil Vaswani,Stavros Volos*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ShieldFS是一个兼容POSIX的机密计算文件系统，通过提交证明、哈希链和嵌入式Merkle树，为持久存储提供端到端的完整性和新鲜性保证，无需修改应用，在基于ZFS的实现ShieldZFS中实现了与最先进文件系统相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机密计算通过可信执行环境（TEEs）保护应用，但持久存储仍面临恶意云提供商的回滚、重放、分叉或篡改磁盘状态等攻击。现有方案要么假设存储可信、要么开销过高、要么将完整性逻辑推给应用，缺乏一个无需应用修改、具有可接受开销的端到端完整性保证方案。

Method: ShieldFS使用简洁密码学承诺表示许可的文件系统状态，承诺维护在TEE内部并复制到轻量级可信注册表中。磁盘上的数据结构（包括预写日志和存储池）通过哈希链和嵌入式Merkle树进行认证。利用事务和写时复制（CoW）原子地更新持久文件系统状态与承诺，并在读取时验证承诺，从而检测回滚、重放和equivocation攻击。通过扩展ZFS实现为ShieldZFS。

Result: 通过标准文件系统基准测试和真实工作负载评估，ShieldZFS在提供强完整性（integrity）和新鲜性（freshness）保证的同时，性能与最先进的文件系统相当。

Conclusion: ShieldFS成功解决了机密计算中持久存储的安全漏洞，在无应用改动的前提下，以可接受的性能开销实现了对恶意云提供商攻击（回滚、重放、分叉、篡改）的鲁棒防护，并验证了该方案的实际可行性与性能竞争力。

Abstract: Confidential computing protects applications inside Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs), but it leaves storage vulnerable. Even with disk encryption, a malicious cloud provider can roll back, replay, fork, or tamper with disk state, breaking the integrity and freshness guarantees required by stateful applications. Existing solutions either assume trusted storage, incur high overheads, or push integrity logic into applications. We present ShieldFS, a POSIX-compliant filesystem that provides end-to-end integrity and freshness for persistent storage in the confidential-computing threat model without requiring application changes. ShieldFS represents permissible filesystem states using succinct cryptographic commitments, maintained inside TEEs and replicated in a lightweight trusted registry. On-disk data structures, including a write-ahead log and a storage pool, are authenticated using hash chains and an embedded Merkle tree. ShieldFS utilizes transactions and copy-on-write to update persistent filesystem state and commitments atomically. The commitments are verified during reads, ensuring that rollback, replay, and equivocation attacks are detected even when the entire I/O stack is untrusted. We implement the design by extending ZFS, yielding ShieldZFS. Evaluation with standard filesystem benchmarks and real-world workloads shows that ShieldZFS provides strong integrity and freshness guarantees with performance comparable to state-of-the-art filesystems.

</details>


### [122] [ShadowPath: Lookup-Private Credential Status Verification over Authenticated State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19937)
*Patrick Herbke,Wolf Rieder,Christian René Sechting,Huaning Yang,Sid Lamichhane,Philip Raschke,Axel Küpper*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ShadowPath是一种可验证凭证撤销检查方案，将凭据状态查询移到持有者端。持有者以零知识方式证明凭据在验证者选定的注册表根下未被撤销，验证者只得知状态结果而不暴露可观察的元数据。论文首次评估了Verkle树在凭据撤销中的应用，并与稀疏Merkle树对比，发现更短的认证路径并不必然带来更便宜的零知识证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可验证凭证的撤销检查机制会暴露重复的标识符、注册表位置或请求元数据，这些信息可作为稳定句柄用于链接不同的展示，破坏持有者的隐私模型。需要一个不泄露可关联元数据的撤销检查方法。

Method: ShadowPath将凭据状态查找移到持有者端。每次展示时，持有者在零知识下证明凭据在验证者选择的注册表根下未被撤销。论文设计了基于Groth16的证明系统，并首次评估了对Verkle树用于凭据撤销的适用性，与稀疏Merkle树进行对比，使用KZG-based认证。

Result: 在30次桌面试验中，Groth16证明中位耗时：稀疏Merkle树371.6ms，Verkle树2.11s；验证分别耗时3.70ms和7.55ms。在两款主流移动设备上，Groth16 Verkle证明约需3s。结果显示更短的认证路径（Verkle树）并不必然带来更便宜的零知识证明。

Conclusion: ShadowPath在新鲜会话随机性下，验证者可见的状态数据不会揭示两次展示是否使用同一凭据（基于会话值独立性的假设），该保证排除了签发者-验证者合谋和同步流量的情况。Verkle树的更短认证路径在零知识证明成本上并不占优。

Abstract: Verifiable credentials let holders present digitally signed claims without requiring the issuer to participate in every presentation. Revocation complicates this privacy model because a verifier must determine whether a credential remains valid. Existing status checks may expose recurring identifiers, registry positions, or request metadata. Such information can serve as stable handles to link separate presentations. ShadowPath moves the credential status lookup to the holder. For each presentation, the holder proves, in zero-knowledge, that the credential has not been revoked under the verifier-selected registry root. The verifier learns the status result but not observable metadata. To the best of our knowledge, we provide the first evaluation of Verkle trees for credential revocation and compare them with sparse Merkle trees to assess their applicability in real world applications. The comparison tests whether reducing path depth with Verkle trees offsets the higher cost of KZG-based authentication. Across 30 desktop trials, median Groth16 proving took 371.6ms with sparse Merkle and 2.11s with Verkle. Verification took 3.70ms and 7.55ms, respectively. Groth16 Verkle proving took about 3s on both primary mobile devices. The results show that shorter authenticated paths do not necessarily yield cheaper zero-knowledge proofs. With fresh session randomness, verifier-visible status data do not reveal whether two presentations use the same credential under the stated assumption of session-value independence. This guarantee excludes issuer-verifier collusion and synchronization traffic.

</details>


### [123] [From Noise to Signal: Improving Security Log Anomaly Detection Using LLMs with Endpoint-Specific Logs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19938)
*Christopher Henshaw,Gour Karmakar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于指令微调LLM的标准分类框架，用于检测异常认证行为（包括边缘案例）。在受控网络安全测试床上生成端点特定的认证数据集，评估了三种指令微调LLM（Meta Llama 3.1 8B Instruct、Qwen 2.5 7B Instruct、GPT-OSS 20B）与Wazuh规则检测和OpenSearch统计异常检测的性能对比。Meta Llama 3.1 8B Instruct综合表现最佳（准确率89.3%、召回率88.2%、F1分数91.8%），远超传统方法（Wazuh准确率52%、OpenSearch准确率49.3%），且在边缘异常场景检测中优势显著（80% vs 20% vs 15%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异常行为日志检测方法（如Wazuh基于预定义规则、OpenSearch基于统计异常检测）存在局限性；基于LLM的方法虽能利用语义和上下文信息，但受限于提示词构造、日志数据噪声以及缺乏端点特定认证行为的通用数据集。作者旨在解决这些限制，提升边缘案例下的异常认证行为检测能力。

Method: 研究构建了标准化指令集LLM分类框架，并开发受控网络安全测试床以生成端点特定的认证数据，形成包含正常、边缘和异常行为场景的精挑数据集。评估了三种指令微调的LLM（Meta Llama 3.1 8B Instruct、Qwen 2.5 7B Instruct和GPT-OSS 20B），并与Wazuh规则检测和OpenSearch异常检测在统一的地面真值严重性框架下进行比较。

Result: Meta Llama 3.1 8B Instruct实现最优端到端检测性能：准确率89.3%、召回率88.2%、F1分数91.8%、假阴性率11.8%；检测出80%的边缘异常场景。对比之下，Wazuh准确率52.0%、假阴性率68.6%，OpenSearch准确率49.3%、假阴性率74.5%；两者对边缘异常场景检出率仅为20%和15%。Qwen取得了最低的平均推理延迟和100%的结构化响应有效性，GPT-OSS在产生有效响应时表现出强分类性能。

Conclusion: 指令微调LLM在异常认证行为检测上显著优于传统基于规则和统计的检测方法，尤其在处理边缘案例时优势明显。Meta Llama 3.1 8B Instruct是最佳整体检测方案；不同模型各有优势（Qwen以低延迟和响应有效性见长，GPT-OSS在有效响应下性能突出），可根据部署需求选择合适模型。

Abstract: Existing approaches to anomalous behaviour log detection, such as Wazuh rely primarily on predefined detection rules, while statistical anomaly detection approaches such as OpenSearch identify deviations from previously observed behavioural patterns. Recent research has investigated LLMs for log anomaly detection because of their ability to interpret semantic and contextual information. However, LLM-based approaches can be affected by prompt construction, noisy log data, and reliance on generic datasets that may lack endpoint-specific authentication behaviours. To address these limitations, this study develops a standardised instruction-based LLM classification framework for detecting anomalous authentication behaviours, including borderline cases. A controlled cybersecurity testbed was developed to generate endpoint-specific authentication data, producing a curated dataset comprising normal, borderline, and anomalous behavioural scenarios. Three instruction-tuned LLMs, Meta Llama 3.1 8B Instruct, Qwen 2.5 7B Instruct, and GPT-OSS 20B, were evaluated against Wazuh rule-based detection and OpenSearch Anomaly Detection using a common ground-truth severity framework. Meta Llama 3.1 8B Instruct achieved the strongest overall end-to-end detection performance, with an accuracy of 89.3%, recall of 88.2%, F1-score of 91.8%, and false negative rate of 11.8%. In comparison, Wazuh achieved an accuracy of 52.0% and false negative rate of 68.6%, while OpenSearch achieved an accuracy of 49.3% and false negative rate of 74.5%. Meta Llama also detected 80% of the borderline anomalous scenarios, compared with 20% for Wazuh and 15% for OpenSearch. Qwen achieved lower overall detection performance than Meta Llama but recorded the lowest average inference latency and 100% structured-response validity. GPT-OSS demonstrated strong classification performance when valid responses were produced.

</details>


### [124] [Tracking the Trend in How Speech Synthesizers Deceive People](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19959)
*Milan Šalko,Anton Firc,Kamil Malinka,Vojtěch Staněk,Martin Perešini,Filip Pleško,Jakub Reš*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本研究对比了人类IT专业人员与六个预训练检测器在三种不同年份（2019、2022、2024）语音合成工具生成的深度伪造音频上的识别性能。结果表明，面对最新合成工具（ElevenLabs）时人类F1分数从90%骤降至48%；在部分篡改场景下严格准确率仅9%，且77%的伪造句被误判为真实。人类与检测器的失败模式互补，均无法可靠定位短片段篡改，同时人类对真实语音的误报率也在上升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期研究报道人类以70-80%的准确率检测深度伪造语音，但这些研究主要基于较旧的语音合成器。随着2024年新合成工具的出现，这些结论可能不再适用，需要重新评估人类感知与自动检测器在现代及部分伪造条件下的实际表现。

Method: 研究者选取了2019年（RTVC）、2022年（YourTTS）和2024年（ElevenLabs）三种具代表性的语音合成工具，以82名IT专业人员为参与者，将其识别表现与六个预训练检测器在相同测试材料上进行基准对比。测试包含完全伪造（full spoofs）和部分伪造（partial spoofs，仅替换句子中某一句话）两种条件。

Result: 完全伪造条件下，人类对RTVC和YourTTS的F1分数约为90%，但对ElevenLabs骤降至48%，即便明确告知存在深度伪造。部分伪造条件下，严格准确率仅9%，77%的伪造句被误判为真实语音。同时，人类对真实语音的误报率上升，且人类与检测器的失败模式呈互补性，两者均无法可靠定位短篡改片段。

Conclusion: 人类感知在应对现代合成工具及部分伪造场景时不可靠，需结合程序化验证、来源追踪（provenance）、数字水印及片段级检测等多层次防护手段来应对深度伪造语音的威胁。

Abstract: Advances in speech synthesis have made deepfake audio highly realistic. Earlier studies reported 70-80% human detection accuracy, but relied primarily on older synthesizers. We compare human detection for three selected voice synthesis tools released in 2019, 2022, and 2024 with 82 IT professionals, and benchmark humans against six pretrained detectors on the same material. For fully synthetic speech (full spoofs), the F1 score drops from about 90% for RTVC and YourTTS to 48% for ElevenLabs, although listeners were explicitly warned that deepfakes were present. For partial spoofing, where only one sentence of an utterance is altered, strict accuracy falls to 9%, and listeners classify the synthetic sentence as bona fide 77% of the time. Humans and detectors fail in complementary ways, and neither reliably localizes short manipulations. Additionally, listeners increasingly mislabel bona fide speech as fake, eroding trust in unmanipulated audio. These findings show that human perception alone is unreliable for the selected modern and partial-spoof conditions and motivate procedural verification, provenance, watermarking, and segment-level detection.

</details>


### [125] [COPA: Continual Preference Optimization for Adaptive Prompt Injection Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19982)
*Roshan Sood,Onat Gungor,Tajana Rosing*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出COPA框架，将提示注入防御视为终身学习问题，通过GRPO优化和margin-weighted经验回放实现持续自适应防御，在终身攻击流上相比SOTA防御平均降低攻击成功率4.4倍（最高6.3倍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM提示注入防御多为静态方法，依赖固定的对齐目标或攻击特定的过滤机制，无法应对持续演化的自适应攻击者。虽然近期出现了面向用户偏好漂移的终身对齐方法，但未考虑会利用已学防御弱点不断演化的对手。在真实部署中攻击分布持续变化，需要在不牺牲对既有威胁防护能力的前提下持续适应新攻击。

Method: COPA（Continual Preference Optimization）框架：将提示注入防御建模为终身学习问题。核心组件包括：(1) 基于GRPO（Group Relative Policy Optimization）的优化方式，从新观测到的攻击中增量式整合反馈；(2) margin-weighted experience replay机制，通过加权重放先前攻击类别的经验以保留对旧攻击的防御能力。框架在持续适应新威胁的同时缓解灾难性遗忘，并保持模型的通用能力。

Result: 在终身提示注入攻击流上，COPA相比最先进的防御方法将攻击成功率平均降低4.4倍，最高可达6.3倍，有效证明了持续偏好优化在防御自适应攻击者方面的优越性。

Conclusion: 持续偏好优化是防御LLM自适应攻击者的有效范式。将提示注入防御纳入终身学习框架，能够在对抗环境持续变化的场景中提供稳健、可持续的防护能力，为LLM安全对齐提供了新的研究方向和实用路径。

Abstract: LLMs remain vulnerable to prompt injection attacks, where adversarial instructions embedded in user inputs or external content manipulate model behavior and bypass safeguards. Existing defenses are predominantly static, relying on fixed alignment objectives or attack-specific filtering mechanisms that require redesign as new attack strategies emerge. While recent lifelong alignment methods address shifting user preferences, they do not account for adaptive adversaries that continually evolve to exploit weaknesses in previously learned defenses. This limitation is particularly important in real-world deployments, where evolving attack distributions necessitate continual adaptation without sacrificing robustness to previously encountered threats. We present COPA, a continual preference optimization framework that treats prompt-injection defense as a lifelong learning problem. Instead of one-time alignment, COPA incrementally incorporates feedback from newly observed attacks via GRPO-based optimization and uses margin-weighted experience replay to retain defenses against prior attack classes. This enables continuous adaptation to emerging threats while mitigating catastrophic forgetting and preserving general-purpose model capabilities. Across lifelong prompt injection attack streams, COPA reduces attack success rate by up to 6.3x and 4.4x on average compared to state-of-the-art defenses. These results highlight continual preference optimization as an effective paradigm for defending LLMs against adaptive adversaries.

</details>


### [126] [EchoCoT: Extracting Hidden Chain-of-Thought from Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20055)
*Yiting Qu,Ziqing Yang,Chi Cui,Ye Leng,Junjie Chu,Yang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了从黑盒大型推理模型（LRMs）中通过API交互近乎逐字提取隐藏思维链（CoT）的可能性，提出了EchoCoT多步攻击方法，利用工具调用之间的推理重放表面和API返回的保真信号来自动优化通用注入轨迹，并在多个开源与专有前沿模型上验证了该攻击的有效性，揭示了隐藏CoT提取是实际存在的安全风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿专有大型推理模型（LRMs）的隐藏思维链（CoT）轨迹是极具价值的模型资产，但先前研究未系统探索这些隐藏CoT是否能通过黑盒API交互被直接提取。作者旨在填补这一空白，评估隐藏CoT被逐字提取的安全风险。

Method: 作者首先识别了工具调用之间一个先前被忽视的"推理重放表面"（reasoning replay surface），在此基础上开发了EchoCoT多步攻击方法，利用API返回的保真信号（fidelity signals）迭代提取隐藏CoT。此外，作者还构建了一个基于LLM的优化框架，自动搜索跨数据集有效的通用注入轨迹（universal injection trajectory）。

Result: 在开源LRMs上，EchoCoT实现了最高66.4%的近乎逐字提取成功率，提取的轨迹长度与目标相差不超过10%，且至少90%的token与目标CoT完全匹配。同一注入轨迹可泛化到未见过数据集，在该标准下提取成功率最高达80%。对于前沿专有LRMs，相当比例的提取CoT与供应商报告的推理长度及可用CoT摘要高度一致。EchoCoT还能提取超长CoT：在Gemini-2.5上从32,948 token的目标中成功提取了33,463个token。

Conclusion: 隐藏CoT提取被确认为一项实际的安全风险，凸显了更好保护隐藏CoT资产的必要性。该研究为理解黑盒推理模型的安全边界提供了重要依据，并为未来防御措施的设计指明了方向。

Abstract: Hidden chain-of-thought (CoT) traces, especially those from frontier proprietary large reasoning models (LRMs), are valuable model assets. Yet whether these hidden CoTs can be directly extracted from black-box models remains largely unexplored. In this work, we systematically study whether hidden CoTs can be extracted near-verbatim from black-box LRMs through API interactions. We identify a previously overlooked reasoning replay surface between tool calls and develop EchoCoT, a multi-step attack that iteratively extracts hidden CoTs using API-returned fidelity signals. We further develop an LLM-based optimization framework that automatically searches for an effective universal injection trajectory across various datasets. We evaluate EchoCoT on three open-source and five frontier proprietary LRMs. On open-source LRMs, EchoCoT achieves up to 66.4\% near-verbatim extraction success, with the extracted trace length within 10\% of the target and at least 90\% of tokens exactly matching the target CoT. The same injection trajectory also generalizes to unseen datasets, achieving up to 80\% extraction success under the same criterion. For tested frontier proprietary LRMs, a substantial fraction of extracted CoTs closely align with provider-reported reasoning lengths and available CoT summaries. EchoCoT can also extract very long CoTs: on Gemini-2.5, it extracts 33,463 tokens from a 32,948-token target. These results establish hidden-CoT extraction as a practical security risk and highlight the need to better protect hidden CoT assets.

</details>


### [127] [TrustRAG: Blockchain-Enhanced RAG via Committee-Based Credibility Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20097)
*Baixiang Liu,Haotian Che,Yuan Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TrustRAG是一个基于委员会和区块链的检索增强生成（RAG）系统，通过零知识协议和多方安全计算为文档生成可验证的信任分数，并利用跨链哈希承诺确保文档和评分不可篡改，从而解决RAG系统中数据来源不可验证和信任缺失的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统依赖集中式数据库，缺乏对文档来源、完整性和可信度的有效监督，在医疗、金融、物流和法律等关键领域，篡改或错误文档可能导致严重决策失误。

Method: TrustRAG采用委员会机制：领域专家组成委员会通过零知识协议对文档进行认证，专家的隐藏评分通过安全多方计算（MPC）合成为客户端可验证的信任分数；文档数据和信任分数通过跨链哈希承诺共同维护，任何改动都会被检测，排名可独立重放验证。

Result: 系统实现了文档可信分数可验证、数据不可篡改、排名可独立审计的能力，解决了传统RAG系统中缺乏来源可追溯性和信任保证的问题。

Conclusion: TrustRAG通过将区块链技术与密码学协议相结合，为RAG系统提供了从文档认证到检索排名的全链路信任保障，适用于对内容时效性和准确性高度敏感的领域。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) lets Large Language Models (LLMs) pull in up-to-date, domain-specific information instead of relying only on what they were trained on. Yet most RAG systems still draw from centralized databases with limited oversight, making it difficult to verify where a document came from, whether it has been tampered with, or whether it should be trusted at all. This is a serious problem in domains where both the timeliness and accuracy of retrieved content are critical, such as healthcare, finance, logistics, and legal case law, where a wrong or manipulated document can directly lead to bad decisions.
  We present TrustRAG, a committee-based, blockchain-backed RAG system: before a document is used, it is certified by a committee of domain experts through a zero-knowledge protocol, and the committee's hidden scores are combined via secure multi-party computation into a trust score that any client can verify. These scores, along with the underlying document data, are maintained jointly across chains through hash commitments, so no document or score can be silently altered or dropped, and every ranking can be independently replayed and checked.

</details>


### [128] [Chameleon: Robust Defense Against Tor Website Fingerprinting via Many-to-Many Traffic Morphing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20160)
*Yuwen Cui,Kai Wei,Kehan Shen,Ning Wang,Zhuo Lu,Yao Liu,Guangjing Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Chameleon是一种基于多对多随机流量变形（morphing）的稳健网站指纹（WF）防御方法。它通过选择高类内多样性和低类间差异的变形候选，随机将每个网页映射到多个候选目标，并允许不同网页共享变形目标来增加对抗不确定性。Chameleon引入了基于radix-trie的同步机制，使可插拔传输（PT）端点能通过包方向前缀识别一致的变形轨迹，同时采用轨迹突变和归一化前缀匹配来降低开销。实验表明，Chameleon在封闭和开放世界设置下、三个公开数据集上，相比Adaptive Tamaraw将对抗训练攻击准确率降低最高36.74%，带宽和时间开销分别降低34.12%和60.38%，并能有效抵御基于防御感知自编码器（DAAE）的攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大多数WF防御方法会创建可学习的网页轨迹映射特征；此外，对对抗训练的鲁棒性并不能保证对基于防御感知自编码器（DAAE）攻击的鲁棒性。

Method: Chameleon采用多对多随机流量变形，选择高类内多样性和低类间差异的变形候选，随机将网页映射到多个候选同时允许不同网页共享目标以增加对抗不确定性。它引入基于radix-trie的同步机制配合包方向前缀使PT端点识别一致轨迹，并通过轨迹突变和归一化前缀匹配降低开销。

Result: 对比六种先进防御和五种WF攻击（三个公开数据集、封闭/开放世界）。相比Adaptive Tamaraw，对抗训练攻击准确率降低最多36.74%，带宽和时间开销分别降低34.12%和60.38%。在DAAE的RF攻击下，Chameleon将攻击性能限制在35.19% F1，而Adaptive Tamaraw仅为88.22%。实际PT桥接部署中仅增加16.25%时间开销。

Conclusion: Chameleon是一种稳健且实用的WF防御方案，通过多对多随机变形有效增加对抗不确定性，显著优于现有防御（如Adaptive Tamaraw），尤其在抵御DAAE攻击方面表现突出，且在真实Tor PT部署中开销可控。

Abstract: Website fingerprinting (WF) attacks can infer users' browsing activities from encrypted Tor traffic by exploiting side-channel features. Although many WF defenses have been proposed, we find that most existing defenses create learnable web trace mapping features. We further show that robustness against adversarial training does not necessarily imply robustness against defense-aware autoencoder (DAAE)-based attacks.
  To address these limitations, we present Chameleon, a robust WF defense based on many-to-many randomized traffic morphing. Chameleon selects morphing candidates with high intra-class diversity and low inter-class disparity. Chameleon randomly maps each webpage trace to multiple candidates, and allows different webpages to share morphing targets, thereby increasing adversarial uncertainty. For practical Tor deployment, Chameleon introduces a radix-trie-based synchronization mechanism that enables pluggable transport (PT) endpoints to identify consistent morphing traces using packet-direction prefixes, together with trace mutation and normalized prefix matching to reduce overhead. We evaluate Chameleon against six state-of-the-art defenses and five WF attacks on three public datasets in closed- and open-world settings. Compared with Adaptive Tamaraw, Chameleon reduces adversarial-training-based attack accuracy by up to 36.74% while reducing bandwidth and time overhead by 34.12% and 60.38%, respectively. Under DAAE-based RF attacks on GTT23, Chameleon limits attack performance to 35.19% F1-score while Adaptive Tamaraw only limits it to 88.22% F1-score. In the real-world PT bridge evaluation, Chameleon substantially reduces the effectiveness of strong WF attacks while incurring only 16.25% time overhead.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [129] [TorchDCM: A Unified PyTorch-Native Package for Discrete Choice Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19231)
*Baichuan Mo,Zhengzhong Ricky You,Xiqun Michael Chen,Ruimin Li*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出TorchDCM，一个基于PyTorch的开源离散选择模型估计包，将计量经济建模编译为统一的PyTorch原生的似然引擎，支持CPU/CUDA，覆盖Biogeme和Apollo的主要功能。在45个合成案例中全部完成且速度最快，中位运行时间较Biogeme和Apollo降低89.1%-99.7%，CUDA提供额外12.0-71.0x加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模且仿真密集的离散选择模型需要反复评估效用、概率、导数及模拟似然，现有DCM软件（如Biogeme、Apollo）提供了成熟的计量经济学工作流，但面向GPU的工具只加速了部分模型，在计量覆盖范围与可扩展的微分计算之间存在明显鸿沟。

Method: TorchDCM将选择数据和模型规格编译为统一的PyTorch原生似然引擎，用于估计、推断、预测和结构化报告，可运行于CPU或CUDA设备。覆盖多项、嵌套、混合、有序、潜变量和面板似然等主要功能，支持不规则的选项集（ragged choice sets）、参数约束、协方差估计、支付意愿分析、弹性及可扩展似然组件。

Result: 在7个其他估计包的对比中，TorchDCM完成全部45个合成案例，在所有可比较的合成案例中运行最快，并满足所有至少有两个可比解场景的预设最终对数似然容差。相比Biogeme和Apollo，中位运行时间减少89.1%-99.7%；CUDA比单核TorchDCM提供额外12.0-71.0x加速。

Conclusion: TorchDCM建立了可扩展、可复现的计量经济估计与可微选择模型开发的基础，源码与示例已在GitHub开源。

Abstract: Estimating large and simulation-intensive discrete choice models (DCMs) requires repeated evaluation of utilities, probabilities, derivatives, and simulated likelihoods over many observations, alternatives, and draws. Existing DCM software provides mature econometric workflows, while recent GPU-oriented tools accelerate selected models, leaving a gap between econometric coverage and scalable differentiable computation. We introduce TorchDCM, an open Python package for discrete choice modeling that compiles choice data and model specifications into a unified PyTorch-native likelihood engine for estimation, inference, prediction, and structured reporting on CPU or CUDA devices. The package covers the principal econometric functionality available across Biogeme and Apollo, including multinomial, nested, mixed, ordered, latent-variable, and panel likelihoods. It also supports ragged choice sets, constrained parameters, covariance estimation, willingness-to-pay analysis, elasticities, and extensible likelihood components. We evaluate TorchDCM against seven other estimation packages in aligned synthetic and real-data full-estimation experiments. TorchDCM completes all 45 synthetic cases, runs fastest in every comparable synthetic case, and satisfies the prespecified final-log-likelihood tolerance in every comparison with at least two comparable solutions. More precisely, it reduces median runtime by 89.1%-99.7% relative to Biogeme and Apollo across model-data settings. CUDA provides an additional 12.0-71.0x speedup over single-core TorchDCM. These results establish a scalable and reproducible foundation for econometric estimation and differentiable choice-model development. The open-source package and executed examples are available at https://github.com/mbc96325/torchdcm.

</details>


### [130] [Discrete Diffusion Inference-Time Control with Nested Sequential Monte Carlo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20123)
*Lohithsai Yadala Chanchu,Hany Abdulsamad,Christian A. Naesseth*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究离散扩散语言模型中的推理时控制问题，提出了嵌套序贯蒙特卡洛（NSMC）和完全自适应的NSMC（FA-NSMC）方法，用于在无需重新训练的情况下将采样引导至序列级奖励，并纠正了先前方法中的偏差问题，在毒性和流畅性控制任务上优于现有基线。
